var XMLBuilder = function() {
    this.root = null;
    this.current = null;
    this.createElement = function(name) {
        var el = { name: name, attributes: {}, children: [], text: "" };
        if (!this.root) this.root = el;
        return el;
    };
    this.element = function(name, attrs, text) {
        var el = this.createElement(name);
        if (attrs) el.attributes = attrs;
        if (text) el.text = text;
        if (this.current) this.current.children.push(el);
        return el;
    };
    this.appendTo = function(parent) { this.current = parent; return this; };
    this.addAttribute = function(name, value) {
        if (this.current) this.current.attributes[name] = value;
        return this;
    };
    this.setText = function(text) {
        if (this.current) this.current.text = text;
        return this;
    };
    this.build = function(node) {
        node = node || this.root;
        var xml = "<" + node.name;
        for (var attr in node.attributes) xml += " " + attr + "=\"" + node.attributes[attr] + "\"";
        if (node.children.length === 0 && node.text === "") {
            xml += " />";
        } else {
            xml += ">";
            if (node.text) xml += node.text;
            for (var i = 0; i < node.children.length; i++) xml += this.build(node.children[i]);
            xml += "</" + node.name + ">";
        }
        return xml;
    };
    this.toString = function() { return this.build(); };
};
var JSONBuilder = function() {
    this.data = null;
    this.obj = function() { this.data = {}; return this; };
    this.arr = function() { this.data = []; return this; };
    this.set = function(key, value) { if (this.data !== null) this.data[key] = value; return this; };
    this.push = function(value) { if (this.data instanceof Array) this.data.push(value); return this; };
    this.key = function(key) { return new JSONBuilder().obj().set(key, this.data); };
    this.build = function() { return new JSONOps().stringify(this.data); };
};
var URLBuilder = function() {
    this.protocol = "";
    this.host = "";
    this.port = "";
    this.path = "";
    this.queryParams = {};
    this.hash = "";
    this.setProtocol = function(protocol) { this.protocol = protocol; return this; };
    this.setHost = function(host) { this.host = host; return this; };
    this.setPort = function(port) { this.port = port; return this; };
    this.setPath = function(path) { this.path = path; return this; };
    this.addQueryParam = function(key, value) { this.queryParams[key] = value; return this; };
    this.setHash = function(hash) { this.hash = hash; return this; };
    this.build = function() {
        var url = "";
        if (this.protocol) url += this.protocol + "://";
        if (this.host) url += this.host;
        if (this.port) url += ":" + this.port;
        if (this.path) url += this.path;
        var first = true;
        for (var key in this.queryParams) {
            url += (first ? "?" : "&") + key + "=" + encodeURIComponent(this.queryParams[key]);
            first = false;
        }
        if (this.hash) url += "#" + this.hash;
        return url;
    };
};
var RangeUtils = function() {
    this.range = function(start, end, step) {
        step = step || 1;
        var result = [];
        if (step > 0) {
            for (var i = start; i < end; i += step) result.push(i);
        } else {
            for (var i = start; i > end; i += step) result.push(i);
        }
        return result;
    };
    this.rangeTo = function(end) { return this.range(0, end, 1); };
    this.repeat = function(value, times) {
        var result = [];
        for (var i = 0; i < times; i++) result.push(value);
        return result;
    };
    this.fill = function(size, value) { return this.repeat(value, size); };
    this.zip = function(arr1, arr2) {
        var result = [];
        var len = Math.min(arr1.length, arr2.length);
        for (var i = 0; i < len; i++) result.push([arr1[i], arr2[i]]);
        return result;
    };
    this.unzip = function(pairs) {
        var arr1 = [], arr2 = [];
        for (var i = 0; i < pairs.length; i++) { arr1.push(pairs[i][0]); arr2.push(pairs[i][1]); }
        return [arr1, arr2];
    };
    this.chunk = function(arr, size) {
        var result = [];
        for (var i = 0; i < arr.length; i += size) result.push(arr.slice(i, i + size));
        return result;
    };
    this.flatten = function(arr) {
        var result = [];
        for (var i = 0; i < arr.length; i++) {
            if (arr[i] instanceof Array) {
                var flat = this.flatten(arr[i]);
                for (var j = 0; j < flat.length; j++) result.push(flat[j]);
            } else { result.push(arr[i]); }
        }
        return result;
    };
    this.difference = function(arr1, arr2) {
        var result = [];
        for (var i = 0; i < arr1.length; i++) {
            if (arr2.indexOf(arr1[i]) < 0) result.push(arr1[i]);
        }
        return result;
    };
    this.intersection = function(arr1, arr2) {
        var result = [];
        for (var i = 0; i < arr1.length; i++) {
            if (arr2.indexOf(arr1[i]) >= 0) result.push(arr1[i]);
        }
        return result;
    };
    this.union = function(arr1, arr2) {
        var result = arr1.slice();
        for (var i = 0; i < arr2.length; i++) {
            if (result.indexOf(arr2[i]) < 0) result.push(arr2[i]);
        }
        return result;
    };
};
var NumberSequence = function() {
    this.fibonacci = function(n) {
        if (n <= 1) return [0, 1].slice(0, n + 1);
        var seq = this.fibonacci(n - 1);
        seq.push(seq[n - 1] + seq[n - 2]);
        return seq;
    };
    this.primes = function(max) {
        var sieve = [], primes = [];
        for (var i = 2; i <= max; i++) {
            if (!sieve[i]) {
                primes.push(i);
                for (var j = i * i; j <= max; j += i) sieve[j] = true;
            }
        }
        return primes;
    };
    this.isPrime = function(n) {
        if (n < 2) return false;
        if (n === 2) return true;
        if (n % 2 === 0) return false;
        for (var i = 3; i <= Math.sqrt(n); i += 2) {
            if (n % i === 0) return false;
        }
        return true;
    };
    this.factorial = function(n) {
        if (n <= 1) return 1;
        return n * this.factorial(n - 1);
    };
    this.factors = function(n) {
        var factors = [];
        for (var i = 1; i <= Math.sqrt(n); i++) {
            if (n % i === 0) {
                factors.push(i);
                if (i !== n / i) factors.push(n / i);
            }
        }
        return factors.sort(function(a, b) { return a - b; });
    };
    this.gcd = function(a, b) { return b === 0 ? a : this.gcd(b, a % b); };
    this.lcm = function(a, b) { return (a * b) / this.gcd(a, b); };
    this.isPerfect = function(n) {
        var sum = 0;
        for (var i = 1; i < n; i++) { if (n % i === 0) sum += i; }
        return sum === n;
    };
    this.isArmstrong = function(n) {
        var digits = String(n).split("").map(Number);
        var power = digits.length;
        var sum = digits.reduce(function(s, d) { return s + Math.pow(d, power); }, 0);
        return sum === n;
    };
};
var BitUtils = function() {
    this.setBit = function(num, bit) { return num | (1 << bit); };
    this.clearBit = function(num, bit) { return num & ~(1 << bit); };
    this.toggleBit = function(num, bit) { return num ^ (1 << bit); };
    this.getBit = function(num, bit) { return (num & (1 << bit)) !== 0; };
    this.setBits = function(num, bits) { return num | bits; };
    this.clearBits = function(num, bits) { return num & ~bits; };
    this.testBits = function(num, bits) { return (num & bits) === bits; };
    this.extractBits = function(num, start, length) {
        var mask = (1 << length) - 1;
        return (num >>> start) & mask;
    };
    this.insertBits = function(num, bits, start, length) {
        var mask = (1 << length) - 1;
        num &= ~(mask << start);
        return num | ((bits & mask) << start);
    };
    this.rotateLeft = function(num, bits, width) {
        var mask = (1 << width) - 1;
        num = (num << bits) & mask | (num >>> (width - bits));
        return num & mask;
    };
    this.rotateRight = function(num, bits, width) {
        var mask = (1 << width) - 1;
        num = (num >>> bits) | ((num << (width - bits)) & mask);
        return num & mask;
    };
    this.reverseBits = function(num, width) {
        var result = 0;
        for (var i = 0; i < width; i++) {
            result = (result << 1) | (num & 1);
            num >>>= 1;
        }
        return result;
    };
    this.countSetBits = function(num) {
        var count = 0;
        while (num) { num &= num - 1; count++; }
        return count;
    };
    this.parity = function(num) {
        var parity = 0;
        while (num) { parity ^= (num & 1); num >>>= 1; }
        return parity;
    };
};
var TimeUtils = function() {
    this.now = function() { return Date.now(); };
    this.today = function() {
        var now = new Date();
        return new Date(now.getFullYear(), now.getMonth(), now.getDate());
    };
    this.addDays = function(date, days) {
        var result = new Date(date);
        result.setDate(result.getDate() + days);
        return result;
    };
    this.addMonths = function(date, months) {
        var result = new Date(date);
        result.setMonth(result.getMonth() + months);
        return result;
    };
    this.diffDays = function(date1, date2) {
        var diff = Math.abs(date1 - date2);
        return Math.floor(diff / (1000 * 60 * 60 * 24));
    };
    this.diffHours = function(date1, date2) {
        var diff = Math.abs(date1 - date2);
        return Math.floor(diff / (1000 * 60 * 60));
    };
    this.diffMinutes = function(date1, date2) {
        var diff = Math.abs(date1 - date2);
        return Math.floor(diff / (1000 * 60));
    };
    this.diffSeconds = function(date1, date2) {
        var diff = Math.abs(date1 - date2);
        return Math.floor(diff / 1000);
    };
    this.isLeapYear = function(year) {
        return (year % 4 === 0 && year % 100 !== 0) || (year % 400 === 0);
    };
    this.getDaysInMonth = function(year, month) {
        return new Date(year, month + 1, 0).getDate();
    };
    this.startOfWeek = function(date) {
        var d = new Date(date);
        var day = d.getDay();
        d.setDate(d.getDate() - day);
        return d;
    };
    this.endOfWeek = function(date) {
        var d = new Date(date);
        var day = d.getDay();
        d.setDate(d.getDate() + (6 - day));
        return d;
    };
    this.startOfMonth = function(date) {
        return new Date(date.getFullYear(), date.getMonth(), 1);
    };
    this.endOfMonth = function(date) {
        return new Date(date.getFullYear(), date.getMonth() + 1, 0);
    };
    this.isWeekend = function(date) {
        var day = date.getDay();
        return day === 0 || day === 6;
    };
    this.isSameDay = function(date1, date2) {
        return date1.getFullYear() === date2.getFullYear() &&
               date1.getMonth() === date2.getMonth() &&
               date1.getDate() === date2.getDate();
    };
    this.getWeekNumber = function(date) {
        var d = new Date(Date.UTC(date.getFullYear(), date.getMonth(), date.getDate()));
        d.setUTCDate(d.getUTCDate() + 4 - (d.getUTCDay() || 7));
        var yearStart = new Date(Date.UTC(d.getUTCFullYear(), 0, 1));
        return Math.ceil((((d - yearStart) / 86400000) + 1) / 7);
    };
};
var Stopwatch = function() {
    this.startTime = null;
    this.endTime = null;
    this.running = false;
    this.start = function() {
        this.startTime = Date.now();
        this.running = true;
        return this;
    };
    this.stop = function() {
        if (!this.running) return this;
        this.endTime = Date.now();
        this.running = false;
        return this;
    };
    this.reset = function() {
        this.startTime = null;
        this.endTime = null;
        this.running = false;
        return this;
    };
    this.elapsed = function() {
        if (!this.startTime) return 0;
        if (this.running) return Date.now() - this.startTime;
        if (this.endTime) return this.endTime - this.startTime;
        return 0;
    };
    this.elapsedMs = function() { return this.elapsed(); };
    this.elapsedSeconds = function() { return this.elapsed() / 1000; };
    this.elapsedMinutes = function() { return this.elapsed() / (1000 * 60); };
    this.elapsedHours = function() { return this.elapsed() / (1000 * 60 * 60); };
    this.lap = function() {
        if (!this.startTime) return null;
        var lapTime = this.running ? Date.now() : this.endTime;
        return lapTime - this.startTime;
    };
};
var TimedCache = function() {
    this.cache = {};
    this.expiry = {};
    this.defaultTTL = 60000;
    this.set = function(key, value, ttl) {
        this.cache[key] = value;
        this.expiry[key] = Date.now() + (ttl || this.defaultTTL);
        return this;
    };
    this.get = function(key) {
        if (!this.cache.hasOwnProperty(key)) return null;
        if (Date.now() > this.expiry[key]) {
            this.remove(key);
            return null;
        }
        return this.cache[key];
    };
    this.has = function(key) { return this.get(key) !== null; };
    this.remove = function(key) {
        delete this.cache[key];
        delete this.expiry[key];
    };
    this.clear = function() {
        this.cache = {};
        this.expiry = {};
    };
    this.cleanup = function() {
        var now = Date.now();
        for (var key in this.expiry) {
            if (now > this.expiry[key]) this.remove(key);
        }
    };
    this.size = function() { return Object.keys(this.cache).length; };
};
var LRU = function(maxSize) {
    this.maxSize = maxSize || 100;
    this.cache = {};
    this.order = [];
    this.get = function(key) {
        if (!this.cache.hasOwnProperty(key)) return null;
        var index = this.order.indexOf(key);
        if (index >= 0) {
            this.order.splice(index, 1);
            this.order.push(key);
        }
        return this.cache[key];
    };
    this.set = function(key, value) {
        if (this.cache.hasOwnProperty(key)) {
            var index = this.order.indexOf(key);
            this.order.splice(index, 1);
        } else {
            if (Object.keys(this.cache).length >= this.maxSize) {
                var oldest = this.order.shift();
                delete this.cache[oldest];
            }
        }
        this.cache[key] = value;
        this.order.push(key);
    };
    this.remove = function(key) {
        if (this.cache.hasOwnProperty(key)) {
            var index = this.order.indexOf(key);
            this.order.splice(index, 1);
            delete this.cache[key];
        }
    };
    this.clear = function() {
        this.cache = {};
        this.order = [];
    };
    this.size = function() { return Object.keys(this.cache).length; };
    this.keys = function() { return this.order.slice(); };
};
var BloomFilter = function(size, hashCount) {
    this.size = size || 1000;
    this.hashCount = hashCount || 3;
    this.bitArray = this.fill(false, this.size);
    this.fill = function(val, len) {
        var arr = [];
        for (var i = 0; i < len; i++) arr.push(val);
        return arr;
    };
    this.hash = function(value) {
        var results = [];
        var hash1 = 0, hash2 = 0;
        for (var i = 0; i < value.length; i++) {
            hash1 = (hash1 * 31 + value.charCodeAt(i)) % this.size;
            hash2 = (hash2 * 37 + value.charCodeAt(i)) % this.size;
        }
        for (var i = 0; i < this.hashCount; i++) {
            results.push((hash1 + i * hash2) % this.size);
        }
        return results;
    };
    this.add = function(value) {
        var indices = this.hash(value);
        for (var i = 0; i < indices.length; i++) this.bitArray[indices[i]] = true;
    };
    this.contains = function(value) {
        var indices = this.hash(value);
        for (var i = 0; i < indices.length; i++) {
            if (!this.bitArray[indices[i]]) return false;
        }
        return true;
    };
};
var CombinationsGenerator = function() {
    this.combinations = function(arr, k) {
        if (k === 1) return arr.map(function(v) { return [v]; });
        if (k === arr.length) return [arr];
        if (k > arr.length) return [];
        var result = [];
        for (var i = 0; i <= arr.length - k; i++) {
            var rest = arr.slice(i + 1);
            var combs = this.combinations(rest, k - 1);
            for (var j = 0; j < combs.length; j++) result.push([arr[i]].concat(combs[j]));
        }
        return result;
    };
    this.permutations = function(arr) {
        if (arr.length <= 1) return [arr];
        var result = [];
        for (var i = 0; i < arr.length; i++) {
            var rest = arr.slice(0, i).concat(arr.slice(i + 1));
            var perms = this.permutations(rest);
            for (var j = 0; j < perms.length; j++) result.push([arr[i]].concat(perms[j]));
        }
        return result;
    };
    this.permutationsWithRepetition = function(arr, length) {
        if (length === 1) return arr.map(function(v) { return [v]; });
        var result = [];
        var partial = this.permutationsWithRepetition(arr, length - 1);
        for (var i = 0; i < arr.length; i++) {
            for (var j = 0; j < partial.length; j++) result.push([arr[i]].concat(partial[j]));
        }
        return result;
    };
    this.combinationsWithRepetition = function(arr, k) {
        if (k === 1) return arr.map(function(v) { return [v]; });
        var result = [];
        for (var i = 0; i < arr.length; i++) {
            var rest = arr.slice(i);
            var combs = this.combinationsWithRepetition(rest, k - 1);
            for (var j = 0; j < combs.length; j++) result.push([arr[i]].concat(combs[j]));
        }
        return result;
    };
    this.powerSet = function(arr) {
        var result = [[]];
        for (var i = 0; i < arr.length; i++) {
            var current = [];
            for (var j = 0; j < result.length; j++) current.push(result[j].concat([arr[i]]));
            result = result.concat(current);
        }
        return result;
    };
};
var HeapUtils = function() {
    this.MinHeap = function() {
        this.data = [];
        this.push = function(value) {
            this.data.push(value);
            this.bubbleUp(this.data.length - 1);
        };
        this.pop = function() {
            if (this.data.length === 0) return null;
            var min = this.data[0];
            var last = this.data.pop();
            if (this.data.length > 0) {
                this.data[0] = last;
                this.bubbleDown(0);
            }
            return min;
        };
        this.bubbleUp = function(index) {
            while (index > 0) {
                var parentIndex = Math.floor((index - 1) / 2);
                if (this.data[index] >= this.data[parentIndex]) break;
                var temp = this.data[index];
                this.data[index] = this.data[parentIndex];
                this.data[parentIndex] = temp;
                index = parentIndex;
            }
        };
        this.bubbleDown = function(index) {
            while (true) {
                var smallest = index;
                var left = 2 * index + 1;
                var right = 2 * index + 2;
                if (left < this.data.length && this.data[left] < this.data[smallest]) smallest = left;
                if (right < this.data.length && this.data[right] < this.data[smallest]) smallest = right;
                if (smallest === index) break;
                var temp = this.data[index];
                this.data[index] = this.data[smallest];
                this.data[smallest] = temp;
                index = smallest;
            }
        };
        this.size = function() { return this.data.length; };
        this.isEmpty = function() { return this.data.length === 0; };
    };
    this.MaxHeap = function() {
        this.data = [];
        this.push = function(value) {
            this.data.push(value);
            this.bubbleUp(this.data.length - 1);
        };
        this.pop = function() {
            if (this.data.length === 0) return null;
            var max = this.data[0];
            var last = this.data.pop();
            if (this.data.length > 0) {
                this.data[0] = last;
                this.bubbleDown(0);
            }
            return max;
        };
        this.bubbleUp = function(index) {
            while (index > 0) {
                var parentIndex = Math.floor((index - 1) / 2);
                if (this.data[index] <= this.data[parentIndex]) break;
                var temp = this.data[index];
                this.data[index] = this.data[parentIndex];
                this.data[parentIndex] = temp;
                index = parentIndex;
            }
        };
        this.bubbleDown = function(index) {
            while (true) {
                var largest = index;
                var left = 2 * index + 1;
                var right = 2 * index + 2;
                if (left < this.data.length && this.data[left] > this.data[largest]) largest = left;
                if (right < this.data.length && this.data[right] > this.data[largest]) largest = right;
                if (largest === index) break;
                var temp = this.data[index];
                this.data[index] = this.data[largest];
                this.data[largest] = temp;
                index = largest;
            }
        };
        this.size = function() { return this.data.length; };
        this.isEmpty = function() { return this.data.length === 0; };
    };
    this.heapSort = function(arr) {
        var heap = new this.MinHeap();
        for (var i = 0; i < arr.length; i++) heap.push(arr[i]);
        var result = [];
        while (!heap.isEmpty()) result.push(heap.pop());
        return result;
    };
};
var TrieNode = function() {
    this.children = {};
    this.isEnd = false;
    this.value = null;
};
var TrieUtils = function() {
    this.root = new TrieNode();
    this.insert = function(word, value) {
        var node = this.root;
        for (var i = 0; i < word.length; i++) {
            var char = word[i];
            if (!node.children[char]) node.children[char] = new TrieNode();
            node = node.children[char];
        }
        node.isEnd = true;
        node.value = value;
    };
    this.search = function(word) {
        var node = this.root;
        for (var i = 0; i < word.length; i++) {
            var char = word[i];
            if (!node.children[char]) return null;
            node = node.children[char];
        }
        return node.isEnd ? node.value : null;
    };
    this.startsWith = function(prefix) {
        var node = this.root;
        for (var i = 0; i < prefix.length; i++) {
            var char = prefix[i];
            if (!node.children[char]) return false;
            node = node.children[char];
        }
        return true;
    };
    this.findAll = function(prefix) {
        var node = this.root;
        for (var i = 0; i < prefix.length; i++) {
            var char = prefix[i];
            if (!node.children[char]) return [];
            node = node.children[char];
        }
        var results = [];
        this.collect(node, prefix, results);
        return results;
    };
    this.collect = function(node, current, results) {
        if (node.isEnd) results.push({ word: current, value: node.value });
        for (var char in node.children) {
            this.collect(node.children[char], current + char, results);
        }
    };
    this.remove = function(word) {
        var node = this.root;
        var stack = [];
        for (var i = 0; i < word.length; i++) {
            var char = word[i];
            if (!node.children[char]) return false;
            stack.push({ node: node, char: char });
            node = node.children[char];
        }
        if (!node.isEnd) return false;
        node.isEnd = false;
        node.value = null;
        while (stack.length > 0) {
            var item = stack.pop();
            if (Object.keys(item.node.children[item.char].children).length === 0 && !item.node.children[item.char].isEnd) {
                delete item.node.children[item.char];
            } else break;
        }
        return true;
    };
};
var DisjointSet = function() {
    this.parent = {};
    this.rank = {};
    this.makeSet = function(x) {
        if (!this.parent.hasOwnProperty(x)) {
            this.parent[x] = x;
            this.rank[x] = 0;
        }
    };
    this.find = function(x) {
        if (this.parent[x] !== x) this.parent[x] = this.find(this.parent[x]);
        return this.parent[x];
    };
    this.union = function(x, y) {
        var rootX = this.find(x);
        var rootY = this.find(y);
        if (rootX === rootY) return;
        if (this.rank[rootX] < this.rank[rootY]) this.parent[rootX] = rootY;
        else if (this.rank[rootX] > this.rank[rootY]) this.parent[rootY] = rootX;
        else {
            this.parent[rootY] = rootX;
            this.rank[rootX]++;
        }
    };
    this.connected = function(x, y) { return this.find(x) === this.find(y); };
};
var AVLTree = function() {
    this.root = null;
    this.Node = function(key, value) {
        this.key = key;
        this.value = value;
        this.left = null;
        this.right = null;
        this.height = 1;
    };
    this.height = function(node) { return node ? node.height : 0; };
    this.balanceFactor = function(node) { return node ? this.height(node.left) - this.height(node.right) : 0; };
    this.rotateRight = function(y) {
        var x = y.left;
        var T2 = x.right;
        x.right = y;
        y.left = T2;
        y.height = Math.max(this.height(y.left), this.height(y.right)) + 1;
        x.height = Math.max(this.height(x.left), this.height(x.right)) + 1;
        return x;
    };
    this.rotateLeft = function(x) {
        var y = x.right;
        var T2 = y.left;
        y.left = x;
        x.right = T2;
        x.height = Math.max(this.height(x.left), this.height(x.right)) + 1;
        y.height = Math.max(this.height(y.left), this.height(y.right)) + 1;
        return y;
    };
    this.insert = function(key, value) {
        var node = this.Node(key, value);
        if (!this.root) { this.root = node; return this.root; }
        var current = this.root;
        while (true) {
            if (key < current.key) {
                if (!current.left) { current.left = node; break; }
                current = current.left;
            } else {
                if (!current.right) { current.right = node; break; }
                current = current.right;
            }
        }
        this.rebalance(current);
        return node;
    };
    this.rebalance = function(node) {
        node.height = Math.max(this.height(node.left), this.height(node.right)) + 1;
        var balance = this.balanceFactor(node);
        if (balance > 1) {
            if (this.balanceFactor(node.left) < 0) node.left = this.rotateLeft(node.left);
            return this.rotateRight(node);
        }
        if (balance < -1) {
            if (this.balanceFactor(node.right) > 0) node.right = this.rotateRight(node.right);
            return this.rotateLeft(node);
        }
        return node;
    };
    this.search = function(key) {
        var current = this.root;
        while (current) {
            if (key === current.key) return current.value;
            if (key < current.key) current = current.left;
            else current = current.right;
        }
        return null;
    };
    this.inOrder = function(node, result) {
        result = result || [];
        if (node) {
            this.inOrder(node.left, result);
            result.push({ key: node.key, value: node.value });
            this.inOrder(node.right, result);
        }
        return result;
    };
    this.preOrder = function(node, result) {
        result = result || [];
        if (node) {
            result.push({ key: node.key, value: node.value });
            this.preOrder(node.left, result);
            this.preOrder(node.right, result);
        }
        return result;
    };
    this.postOrder = function(node, result) {
        result = result || [];
        if (node) {
            this.postOrder(node.left, result);
            this.postOrder(node.right, result);
            result.push({ key: node.key, value: node.value });
        }
        return result;
    };
};
var RedBlackTree = function() {
    this.RED = true;
    this.BLACK = false;
    this.root = null;
    this.Node = function(key, value) {
        this.key = key;
        this.value = value;
        this.color = this.RED;
        this.left = null;
        this.right = null;
        this.parent = null;
    };
    this.rotateLeft = function(node) {
        var right = node.right;
        node.right = right.left;
        if (right.left) right.left.parent = node;
        right.parent = node.parent;
        if (!node.parent) this.root = right;
        else if (node === node.parent.left) node.parent.left = right;
        else node.parent.right = right;
        right.left = node;
        node.parent = right;
    };
    this.rotateRight = function(node) {
        var left = node.left;
        node.left = left.right;
        if (left.right) left.right.parent = node;
        left.parent = node.parent;
        if (!node.parent) this.root = left;
        else if (node === node.parent.right) node.parent.right = left;
        else node.parent.left = left;
        left.right = node;
        node.parent = left;
    };
    this.insert = function(key, value) {
        var node = new this.Node(key, value);
        var parent = null;
        var current = this.root;
        while (current) {
            parent = current;
            if (node.key < current.key) current = current.left;
            else current = current.right;
        }
        node.parent = parent;
        if (!parent) this.root = node;
        else if (node.key < parent.key) parent.left = node;
        else parent.right = node;
        this.insertFixup(node);
    };
    this.insertFixup = function(node) {
        while (node.parent && node.parent.color === this.RED) {
            if (node.parent === node.parent.parent.left) {
                var uncle = node.parent.parent.right;
                if (uncle && uncle.color === this.RED) {
                    node.parent.color = this.BLACK;
                    uncle.color = this.BLACK;
                    node.parent.parent.color = this.RED;
                    node = node.parent.parent;
                } else {
                    if (node === node.parent.right) { node = node.parent; this.rotateLeft(node); }
                    node.parent.color = this.BLACK;
                    node.parent.parent.color = this.RED;
                    this.rotateRight(node.parent.parent);
                }
            } else {
                var uncle = node.parent.parent.left;
                if (uncle && uncle.color === this.RED) {
                    node.parent.color = this.BLACK;
                    uncle.color = this.BLACK;
                    node.parent.parent.color = this.RED;
                    node = node.parent.parent;
                } else {
                    if (node === node.parent.left) { node = node.parent; this.rotateRight(node); }
                    node.parent.color = this.BLACK;
                    node.parent.parent.color = this.RED;
                    this.rotateLeft(node.parent.parent);
                }
            }
        }
        this.root.color = this.BLACK;
    };
    this.search = function(key) {
        var current = this.root;
        while (current) {
            if (key === current.key) return current.value;
            if (key < current.key) current = current.left;
            else current = current.right;
        }
        return null;
    };
};
var BTree = function(order) {
    this.order = order || 3;
    this.root = null;
    this.Node = function() {
        this.keys = [];
        this.values = [];
        this.children = [];
        this.leaf = true;
        this.n = 0;
    };
    this.insert = function(key, value) {
        if (!this.root) this.root = new this.Node();
        if (this.root.n === 2 * this.order - 1) {
            var newRoot = new this.Node();
            newRoot.leaf = false;
            newRoot.children.push(this.root);
            this.splitChild(newRoot, 0);
            this.root = newRoot;
        }
        this.insertNonFull(this.root, key, value);
    };
    this.splitChild = function(parent, i) {
        var child = parent.children[i];
        var newChild = new this.Node();
        newChild.leaf = child.leaf;
        for (var j = 0; j < this.order - 1; j++) {
            newChild.keys.push(child.keys[j + this.order]);
            newChild.values.push(child.values[j + this.order]);
        }
        if (!child.leaf) {
            for (var j = 0; j < this.order; j++) newChild.children.push(child.children[j + this.order]);
        }
        child.n = this.order - 1;
        for (var j = parent.n; j > i; j--) parent.children[j + 1] = parent.children[j];
        parent.children[i + 1] = newChild;
        for (var j = parent.n - 1; j >= i; j--) {
            parent.keys[j + 1] = parent.keys[j];
            parent.values[j + 1] = parent.values[j];
        }
        parent.keys[i] = child.keys[this.order - 1];
        parent.values[i] = child.values[this.order - 1];
        parent.n++;
    };
    this.insertNonFull = function(node, key, value) {
        var i = node.n - 1;
        if (node.leaf) {
            while (i >= 0 && key < node.keys[i]) i--;
            i++;
            node.keys.splice(i, 0, key);
            node.values.splice(i, 0, value);
            node.n++;
        } else {
            while (i >= 0 && key < node.keys[i]) i--;
            i++;
            if (node.children[i].n === 2 * this.order - 1) {
                this.splitChild(node, i);
                if (key > node.keys[i]) i++;
            }
            this.insertNonFull(node.children[i], key, value);
        }
    };
    this.search = function(key) {
        return this.searchNode(this.root, key);
    };
    this.searchNode = function(node, key) {
        var i = 0;
        while (i < node.n && key > node.keys[i]) i++;
        if (i < node.n && key === node.keys[i]) return node.values[i];
        if (node.leaf) return null;
        return this.searchNode(node.children[i], key);
    };
};
var SkipListNode = function(key, value) {
    this.key = key;
    this.value = value;
    this.forward = [];
};
var SkipList = function(maxLevel, probability) {
    this.maxLevel = maxLevel || 16;
    this.probability = probability || 0.5;
    this.header = new SkipListNode(null, null);
    this.level = 0;
    this.randomLevel = function() {
        var level = 0;
        while (Math.random() < this.probability && level < this.maxLevel) level++;
        return level;
    };
    this.insert = function(key, value) {
        var update = [];
        var current = this.header;
        for (var i = this.level; i >= 0; i--) {
            while (current.forward[i] && current.forward[i].key < key) current = current.forward[i];
            update[i] = current;
        }
        current = current.forward[0];
        if (current && current.key === key) { current.value = value; return; }
        var newLevel = this.randomLevel();
        if (newLevel > this.level) {
            for (var i = this.level + 1; i <= newLevel; i++) update[i] = this.header;
            this.level = newLevel;
        }
        var newNode = new SkipListNode(key, value);
        for (var i = 0; i <= newLevel; i++) {
            newNode.forward[i] = update[i].forward[i];
            update[i].forward[i] = newNode;
        }
    };
    this.search = function(key) {
        var current = this.header;
        for (var i = this.level; i >= 0; i--) {
            while (current.forward[i] && current.forward[i].key < key) current = current.forward[i];
        }
        current = current.forward[0];
        if (current && current.key === key) return current.value;
        return null;
    };
    this.remove = function(key) {
        var update = [];
        var current = this.header;
        for (var i = this.level; i >= 0; i--) {
            while (current.forward[i] && current.forward[i].key < key) current = current.forward[i];
            update[i] = current;
        }
        current = current.forward[0];
        if (!current || current.key !== key) return;
        for (var i = 0; i <= this.level; i++) {
            if (update[i].forward[i] !== current) break;
            update[i].forward[i] = current.forward[i];
        }
        while (this.level > 0 && !this.header.forward[this.level]) this.level--;
    };
};

var XMLBuilder = function() {
    this.root = null;
    this.current = null;
    this.createElement = function(name) {
        var el = { name: name, attributes: {}, children: [], text: "" };
        if (!this.root) this.root = el;
        return el;
    };
    this.element = function(name, attrs, text) {
        var el = this.createElement(name);
        if (attrs) el.attributes = attrs;
        if (text) el.text = text;
        if (this.current) this.current.children.push(el);
        return el;
    };
    this.appendTo = function(parent) { this.current = parent; return this; };
    this.addAttribute = function(name, value) {
        if (this.current) this.current.attributes[name] = value;
        return this;
    };
    this.setText = function(text) {
        if (this.current) this.current.text = text;
        return this;
    };
    this.build = function(node) {
        node = node || this.root;
        var xml = "<" + node.name;
        for (var attr in node.attributes) xml += " " + attr + "=\"" + node.attributes[attr] + "\"";
        if (node.children.length === 0 && node.text === "") {
            xml += " />";
        } else {
            xml += ">";
            if (node.text) xml += node.text;
            for (var i = 0; i < node.children.length; i++) xml += this.build(node.children[i]);
            xml += "</" + node.name + ">";
        }
        return xml;
    };
    this.toString = function() { return this.build(); };
};
var JSONBuilder = function() {
    this.data = null;
    this.obj = function() { this.data = {}; return this; };
    this.arr = function() { this.data = []; return this; };
    this.set = function(key, value) { if (this.data !== null) this.data[key] = value; return this; };
    this.push = function(value) { if (this.data instanceof Array) this.data.push(value); return this; };
    this.key = function(key) { return new JSONBuilder().obj().set(key, this.data); };
    this.build = function() { return new JSONOps().stringify(this.data); };
};
var URLBuilder = function() {
    this.protocol = "";
    this.host = "";
    this.port = "";
    this.path = "";
    this.queryParams = {};
    this.hash = "";
    this.setProtocol = function(protocol) { this.protocol = protocol; return this; };
    this.setHost = function(host) { this.host = host; return this; };
    this.setPort = function(port) { this.port = port; return this; };
    this.setPath = function(path) { this.path = path; return this; };
    this.addQueryParam = function(key, value) { this.queryParams[key] = value; return this; };
    this.setHash = function(hash) { this.hash = hash; return this; };
    this.build = function() {
        var url = "";
        if (this.protocol) url += this.protocol + "://";
        if (this.host) url += this.host;
        if (this.port) url += ":" + this.port;
        if (this.path) url += this.path;
        var first = true;
        for (var key in this.queryParams) {
            url += (first ? "?" : "&") + key + "=" + encodeURIComponent(this.queryParams[key]);
            first = false;
        }
        if (this.hash) url += "#" + this.hash;
        return url;
    };
};
var RangeUtils = function() {
    this.range = function(start, end, step) {
        step = step || 1;
        var result = [];
        if (step > 0) {
            for (var i = start; i < end; i += step) result.push(i);
        } else {
            for (var i = start; i > end; i += step) result.push(i);
        }
        return result;
    };
    this.rangeTo = function(end) { return this.range(0, end, 1); };
    this.repeat = function(value, times) {
        var result = [];
        for (var i = 0; i < times; i++) result.push(value);
        return result;
    };
    this.fill = function(size, value) { return this.repeat(value, size); };
    this.zip = function(arr1, arr2) {
        var result = [];
        var len = Math.min(arr1.length, arr2.length);
        for (var i = 0; i < len; i++) result.push([arr1[i], arr2[i]]);
        return result;
    };
    this.unzip = function(pairs) {
        var arr1 = [], arr2 = [];
        for (var i = 0; i < pairs.length; i++) { arr1.push(pairs[i][0]); arr2.push(pairs[i][1]); }
        return [arr1, arr2];
    };
    this.chunk = function(arr, size) {
        var result = [];
        for (var i = 0; i < arr.length; i += size) result.push(arr.slice(i, i + size));
        return result;
    };
    this.flatten = function(arr) {
        var result = [];
        for (var i = 0; i < arr.length; i++) {
            if (arr[i] instanceof Array) {
                var flat = this.flatten(arr[i]);
                for (var j = 0; j < flat.length; j++) result.push(flat[j]);
            } else { result.push(arr[i]); }
        }
        return result;
    };
    this.difference = function(arr1, arr2) {
        var result = [];
        for (var i = 0; i < arr1.length; i++) {
            if (arr2.indexOf(arr1[i]) < 0) result.push(arr1[i]);
        }
        return result;
    };
    this.intersection = function(arr1, arr2) {
        var result = [];
        for (var i = 0; i < arr1.length; i++) {
            if (arr2.indexOf(arr1[i]) >= 0) result.push(arr1[i]);
        }
        return result;
    };
    this.union = function(arr1, arr2) {
        var result = arr1.slice();
        for (var i = 0; i < arr2.length; i++) {
            if (result.indexOf(arr2[i]) < 0) result.push(arr2[i]);
        }
        return result;
    };
};
var NumberSequence = function() {
    this.fibonacci = function(n) {
        if (n <= 1) return [0, 1].slice(0, n + 1);
        var seq = this.fibonacci(n - 1);
        seq.push(seq[n - 1] + seq[n - 2]);
        return seq;
    };
    this.primes = function(max) {
        var sieve = [], primes = [];
        for (var i = 2; i <= max; i++) {
            if (!sieve[i]) {
                primes.push(i);
                for (var j = i * i; j <= max; j += i) sieve[j] = true;
            }
        }
        return primes;
    };
    this.isPrime = function(n) {
        if (n < 2) return false;
        if (n === 2) return true;
        if (n % 2 === 0) return false;
        for (var i = 3; i <= Math.sqrt(n); i += 2) {
            if (n % i === 0) return false;
        }
        return true;
    };
    this.factorial = function(n) {
        if (n <= 1) return 1;
        return n * this.factorial(n - 1);
    };
    this.factors = function(n) {
        var factors = [];
        for (var i = 1; i <= Math.sqrt(n); i++) {
            if (n % i === 0) {
                factors.push(i);
                if (i !== n / i) factors.push(n / i);
            }
        }
        return factors.sort(function(a, b) { return a - b; });
    };
    this.gcd = function(a, b) { return b === 0 ? a : this.gcd(b, a % b); };
    this.lcm = function(a, b) { return (a * b) / this.gcd(a, b); };
    this.isPerfect = function(n) {
        var sum = 0;
        for (var i = 1; i < n; i++) { if (n % i === 0) sum += i; }
        return sum === n;
    };
    this.isArmstrong = function(n) {
        var digits = String(n).split("").map(Number);
        var power = digits.length;
        var sum = digits.reduce(function(s, d) { return s + Math.pow(d, power); }, 0);
        return sum === n;
    };
};
var BitUtils = function() {
    this.setBit = function(num, bit) { return num | (1 << bit); };
    this.clearBit = function(num, bit) { return num & ~(1 << bit); };
    this.toggleBit = function(num, bit) { return num ^ (1 << bit); };
    this.getBit = function(num, bit) { return (num & (1 << bit)) !== 0; };
    this.setBits = function(num, bits) { return num | bits; };
    this.clearBits = function(num, bits) { return num & ~bits; };
    this.testBits = function(num, bits) { return (num & bits) === bits; };
    this.extractBits = function(num, start, length) {
        var mask = (1 << length) - 1;
        return (num >>> start) & mask;
    };
    this.insertBits = function(num, bits, start, length) {
        var mask = (1 << length) - 1;
        num &= ~(mask << start);
        return num | ((bits & mask) << start);
    };
    this.rotateLeft = function(num, bits, width) {
        var mask = (1 << width) - 1;
        num = (num << bits) & mask | (num >>> (width - bits));
        return num & mask;
    };
    this.rotateRight = function(num, bits, width) {
        var mask = (1 << width) - 1;
        num = (num >>> bits) | ((num << (width - bits)) & mask);
        return num & mask;
    };
    this.reverseBits = function(num, width) {
        var result = 0;
        for (var i = 0; i < width; i++) {
            result = (result << 1) | (num & 1);
            num >>>= 1;
        }
        return result;
    };
    this.countSetBits = function(num) {
        var count = 0;
        while (num) { num &= num - 1; count++; }
        return count;
    };
    this.parity = function(num) {
        var parity = 0;
        while (num) { parity ^= (num & 1); num >>>= 1; }
        return parity;
    };
};
var TimeUtils = function() {
    this.now = function() { return Date.now(); };
    this.today = function() {
        var now = new Date();
        return new Date(now.getFullYear(), now.getMonth(), now.getDate());
    };
    this.addDays = function(date, days) {
        var result = new Date(date);
        result.setDate(result.getDate() + days);
        return result;
    };
    this.addMonths = function(date, months) {
        var result = new Date(date);
        result.setMonth(result.getMonth() + months);
        return result;
    };
    this.diffDays = function(date1, date2) {
        var diff = Math.abs(date1 - date2);
        return Math.floor(diff / (1000 * 60 * 60 * 24));
    };
    this.diffHours = function(date1, date2) {
        var diff = Math.abs(date1 - date2);
        return Math.floor(diff / (1000 * 60 * 60));
    };
    this.diffMinutes = function(date1, date2) {
        var diff = Math.abs(date1 - date2);
        return Math.floor(diff / (1000 * 60));
    };
    this.diffSeconds = function(date1, date2) {
        var diff = Math.abs(date1 - date2);
        return Math.floor(diff / 1000);
    };
    this.isLeapYear = function(year) {
        return (year % 4 === 0 && year % 100 !== 0) || (year % 400 === 0);
    };
    this.getDaysInMonth = function(year, month) {
        return new Date(year, month + 1, 0).getDate();
    };
    this.startOfWeek = function(date) {
        var d = new Date(date);
        var day = d.getDay();
        d.setDate(d.getDate() - day);
        return d;
    };
    this.endOfWeek = function(date) {
        var d = new Date(date);
        var day = d.getDay();
        d.setDate(d.getDate() + (6 - day));
        return d;
    };
    this.startOfMonth = function(date) {
        return new Date(date.getFullYear(), date.getMonth(), 1);
    };
    this.endOfMonth = function(date) {
        return new Date(date.getFullYear(), date.getMonth() + 1, 0);
    };
    this.isWeekend = function(date) {
        var day = date.getDay();
        return day === 0 || day === 6;
    };
    this.isSameDay = function(date1, date2) {
        return date1.getFullYear() === date2.getFullYear() &&
               date1.getMonth() === date2.getMonth() &&
               date1.getDate() === date2.getDate();
    };
    this.getWeekNumber = function(date) {
        var d = new Date(Date.UTC(date.getFullYear(), date.getMonth(), date.getDate()));
        d.setUTCDate(d.getUTCDate() + 4 - (d.getUTCDay() || 7));
        var yearStart = new Date(Date.UTC(d.getUTCFullYear(), 0, 1));
        return Math.ceil((((d - yearStart) / 86400000) + 1) / 7);
    };
};
var Stopwatch = function() {
    this.startTime = null;
    this.endTime = null;
    this.running = false;
    this.start = function() {
        this.startTime = Date.now();
        this.running = true;
        return this;
    };
    this.stop = function() {
        if (!this.running) return this;
        this.endTime = Date.now();
        this.running = false;
        return this;
    };
    this.reset = function() {
        this.startTime = null;
        this.endTime = null;
        this.running = false;
        return this;
    };
    this.elapsed = function() {
        if (!this.startTime) return 0;
        if (this.running) return Date.now() - this.startTime;
        if (this.endTime) return this.endTime - this.startTime;
        return 0;
    };
    this.elapsedMs = function() { return this.elapsed(); };
    this.elapsedSeconds = function() { return this.elapsed() / 1000; };
    this.elapsedMinutes = function() { return this.elapsed() / (1000 * 60); };
    this.elapsedHours = function() { return this.elapsed() / (1000 * 60 * 60); };
    this.lap = function() {
        if (!this.startTime) return null;
        var lapTime = this.running ? Date.now() : this.endTime;
        return lapTime - this.startTime;
    };
};
var TimedCache = function() {
    this.cache = {};
    this.expiry = {};
    this.defaultTTL = 60000;
    this.set = function(key, value, ttl) {
        this.cache[key] = value;
        this.expiry[key] = Date.now() + (ttl || this.defaultTTL);
        return this;
    };
    this.get = function(key) {
        if (!this.cache.hasOwnProperty(key)) return null;
        if (Date.now() > this.expiry[key]) {
            this.remove(key);
            return null;
        }
        return this.cache[key];
    };
    this.has = function(key) { return this.get(key) !== null; };
    this.remove = function(key) {
        delete this.cache[key];
        delete this.expiry[key];
    };
    this.clear = function() {
        this.cache = {};
        this.expiry = {};
    };
    this.cleanup = function() {
        var now = Date.now();
        for (var key in this.expiry) {
            if (now > this.expiry[key]) this.remove(key);
        }
    };
    this.size = function() { return Object.keys(this.cache).length; };
};
var LRU = function(maxSize) {
    this.maxSize = maxSize || 100;
    this.cache = {};
    this.order = [];
    this.get = function(key) {
        if (!this.cache.hasOwnProperty(key)) return null;
        var index = this.order.indexOf(key);
        if (index >= 0) {
            this.order.splice(index, 1);
            this.order.push(key);
        }
        return this.cache[key];
    };
    this.set = function(key, value) {
        if (this.cache.hasOwnProperty(key)) {
            var index = this.order.indexOf(key);
            this.order.splice(index, 1);
        } else {
            if (Object.keys(this.cache).length >= this.maxSize) {
                var oldest = this.order.shift();
                delete this.cache[oldest];
            }
        }
        this.cache[key] = value;
        this.order.push(key);
    };
    this.remove = function(key) {
        if (this.cache.hasOwnProperty(key)) {
            var index = this.order.indexOf(key);
            this.order.splice(index, 1);
            delete this.cache[key];
        }
    };
    this.clear = function() {
        this.cache = {};
        this.order = [];
    };
    this.size = function() { return Object.keys(this.cache).length; };
    this.keys = function() { return this.order.slice(); };
};
var BloomFilter = function(size, hashCount) {
    this.size = size || 1000;
    this.hashCount = hashCount || 3;
    this.bitArray = this.fill(false, this.size);
    this.fill = function(val, len) {
        var arr = [];
        for (var i = 0; i < len; i++) arr.push(val);
        return arr;
    };
    this.hash = function(value) {
        var results = [];
        var hash1 = 0, hash2 = 0;
        for (var i = 0; i < value.length; i++) {
            hash1 = (hash1 * 31 + value.charCodeAt(i)) % this.size;
            hash2 = (hash2 * 37 + value.charCodeAt(i)) % this.size;
        }
        for (var i = 0; i < this.hashCount; i++) {
            results.push((hash1 + i * hash2) % this.size);
        }
        return results;
    };
    this.add = function(value) {
        var indices = this.hash(value);
        for (var i = 0; i < indices.length; i++) this.bitArray[indices[i]] = true;
    };
    this.contains = function(value) {
        var indices = this.hash(value);
        for (var i = 0; i < indices.length; i++) {
            if (!this.bitArray[indices[i]]) return false;
        }
        return true;
    };
};
var CombinationsGenerator = function() {
    this.combinations = function(arr, k) {
        if (k === 1) return arr.map(function(v) { return [v]; });
        if (k === arr.length) return [arr];
        if (k > arr.length) return [];
        var result = [];
        for (var i = 0; i <= arr.length - k; i++) {
            var rest = arr.slice(i + 1);
            var combs = this.combinations(rest, k - 1);
            for (var j = 0; j < combs.length; j++) result.push([arr[i]].concat(combs[j]));
        }
        return result;
    };
    this.permutations = function(arr) {
        if (arr.length <= 1) return [arr];
        var result = [];
        for (var i = 0; i < arr.length; i++) {
            var rest = arr.slice(0, i).concat(arr.slice(i + 1));
            var perms = this.permutations(rest);
            for (var j = 0; j < perms.length; j++) result.push([arr[i]].concat(perms[j]));
        }
        return result;
    };
    this.permutationsWithRepetition = function(arr, length) {
        if (length === 1) return arr.map(function(v) { return [v]; });
        var result = [];
        var partial = this.permutationsWithRepetition(arr, length - 1);
        for (var i = 0; i < arr.length; i++) {
            for (var j = 0; j < partial.length; j++) result.push([arr[i]].concat(partial[j]));
        }
        return result;
    };
    this.combinationsWithRepetition = function(arr, k) {
        if (k === 1) return arr.map(function(v) { return [v]; });
        var result = [];
        for (var i = 0; i < arr.length; i++) {
            var rest = arr.slice(i);
            var combs = this.combinationsWithRepetition(rest, k - 1);
            for (var j = 0; j < combs.length; j++) result.push([arr[i]].concat(combs[j]));
        }
        return result;
    };
    this.powerSet = function(arr) {
        var result = [[]];
        for (var i = 0; i < arr.length; i++) {
            var current = [];
            for (var j = 0; j < result.length; j++) current.push(result[j].concat([arr[i]]));
            result = result.concat(current);
        }
        return result;
    };
};
var HeapUtils = function() {
    this.MinHeap = function() {
        this.data = [];
        this.push = function(value) {
            this.data.push(value);
            this.bubbleUp(this.data.length - 1);
        };
        this.pop = function() {
            if (this.data.length === 0) return null;
            var min = this.data[0];
            var last = this.data.pop();
            if (this.data.length > 0) {
                this.data[0] = last;
                this.bubbleDown(0);
            }
            return min;
        };
        this.bubbleUp = function(index) {
            while (index > 0) {
                var parentIndex = Math.floor((index - 1) / 2);
                if (this.data[index] >= this.data[parentIndex]) break;
                var temp = this.data[index];
                this.data[index] = this.data[parentIndex];
                this.data[parentIndex] = temp;
                index = parentIndex;
            }
        };
        this.bubbleDown = function(index) {
            while (true) {
                var smallest = index;
                var left = 2 * index + 1;
                var right = 2 * index + 2;
                if (left < this.data.length && this.data[left] < this.data[smallest]) smallest = left;
                if (right < this.data.length && this.data[right] < this.data[smallest]) smallest = right;
                if (smallest === index) break;
                var temp = this.data[index];
                this.data[index] = this.data[smallest];
                this.data[smallest] = temp;
                index = smallest;
            }
        };
        this.size = function() { return this.data.length; };
        this.isEmpty = function() { return this.data.length === 0; };
    };
    this.MaxHeap = function() {
        this.data = [];
        this.push = function(value) {
            this.data.push(value);
            this.bubbleUp(this.data.length - 1);
        };
        this.pop = function() {
            if (this.data.length === 0) return null;
            var max = this.data[0];
            var last = this.data.pop();
            if (this.data.length > 0) {
                this.data[0] = last;
                this.bubbleDown(0);
            }
            return max;
        };
        this.bubbleUp = function(index) {
            while (index > 0) {
                var parentIndex = Math.floor((index - 1) / 2);
                if (this.data[index] <= this.data[parentIndex]) break;
                var temp = this.data[index];
                this.data[index] = this.data[parentIndex];
                this.data[parentIndex] = temp;
                index = parentIndex;
            }
        };
        this.bubbleDown = function(index) {
            while (true) {
                var largest = index;
                var left = 2 * index + 1;
                var right = 2 * index + 2;
                if (left < this.data.length && this.data[left] > this.data[largest]) largest = left;
                if (right < this.data.length && this.data[right] > this.data[largest]) largest = right;
                if (largest === index) break;
                var temp = this.data[index];
                this.data[index] = this.data[largest];
                this.data[largest] = temp;
                index = largest;
            }
        };
        this.size = function() { return this.data.length; };
        this.isEmpty = function() { return this.data.length === 0; };
    };
    this.heapSort = function(arr) {
        var heap = new this.MinHeap();
        for (var i = 0; i < arr.length; i++) heap.push(arr[i]);
        var result = [];
        while (!heap.isEmpty()) result.push(heap.pop());
        return result;
    };
};
var TrieNode = function() {
    this.children = {};
    this.isEnd = false;
    this.value = null;
};
var TrieUtils = function() {
    this.root = new TrieNode();
    this.insert = function(word, value) {
        var node = this.root;
        for (var i = 0; i < word.length; i++) {
            var char = word[i];
            if (!node.children[char]) node.children[char] = new TrieNode();
            node = node.children[char];
        }
        node.isEnd = true;
        node.value = value;
    };
    this.search = function(word) {
        var node = this.root;
        for (var i = 0; i < word.length; i++) {
            var char = word[i];
            if (!node.children[char]) return null;
            node = node.children[char];
        }
        return node.isEnd ? node.value : null;
    };
    this.startsWith = function(prefix) {
        var node = this.root;
        for (var i = 0; i < prefix.length; i++) {
            var char = prefix[i];
            if (!node.children[char]) return false;
            node = node.children[char];
        }
        return true;
    };
    this.findAll = function(prefix) {
        var node = this.root;
        for (var i = 0; i < prefix.length; i++) {
            var char = prefix[i];
            if (!node.children[char]) return [];
            node = node.children[char];
        }
        var results = [];
        this.collect(node, prefix, results);
        return results;
    };
    this.collect = function(node, current, results) {
        if (node.isEnd) results.push({ word: current, value: node.value });
        for (var char in node.children) {
            this.collect(node.children[char], current + char, results);
        }
    };
    this.remove = function(word) {
        var node = this.root;
        var stack = [];
        for (var i = 0; i < word.length; i++) {
            var char = word[i];
            if (!node.children[char]) return false;
            stack.push({ node: node, char: char });
            node = node.children[char];
        }
        if (!node.isEnd) return false;
        node.isEnd = false;
        node.value = null;
        while (stack.length > 0) {
            var item = stack.pop();
            if (Object.keys(item.node.children[item.char].children).length === 0 && !item.node.children[item.char].isEnd) {
                delete item.node.children[item.char];
            } else break;
        }
        return true;
    };
};
var DisjointSet = function() {
    this.parent = {};
    this.rank = {};
    this.makeSet = function(x) {
        if (!this.parent.hasOwnProperty(x)) {
            this.parent[x] = x;
            this.rank[x] = 0;
        }
    };
    this.find = function(x) {
        if (this.parent[x] !== x) this.parent[x] = this.find(this.parent[x]);
        return this.parent[x];
    };
    this.union = function(x, y) {
        var rootX = this.find(x);
        var rootY = this.find(y);
        if (rootX === rootY) return;
        if (this.rank[rootX] < this.rank[rootY]) this.parent[rootX] = rootY;
        else if (this.rank[rootX] > this.rank[rootY]) this.parent[rootY] = rootX;
        else {
            this.parent[rootY] = rootX;
            this.rank[rootX]++;
        }
    };
    this.connected = function(x, y) { return this.find(x) === this.find(y); };
};
var AVLTree = function() {
    this.root = null;
    this.Node = function(key, value) {
        this.key = key;
        this.value = value;
        this.left = null;
        this.right = null;
        this.height = 1;
    };
    this.height = function(node) { return node ? node.height : 0; };
    this.balanceFactor = function(node) { return node ? this.height(node.left) - this.height(node.right) : 0; };
    this.rotateRight = function(y) {
        var x = y.left;
        var T2 = x.right;
        x.right = y;
        y.left = T2;
        y.height = Math.max(this.height(y.left), this.height(y.right)) + 1;
        x.height = Math.max(this.height(x.left), this.height(x.right)) + 1;
        return x;
    };
    this.rotateLeft = function(x) {
        var y = x.right;
        var T2 = y.left;
        y.left = x;
        x.right = T2;
        x.height = Math.max(this.height(x.left), this.height(x.right)) + 1;
        y.height = Math.max(this.height(y.left), this.height(y.right)) + 1;
        return y;
    };
    this.insert = function(key, value) {
        var node = this.Node(key, value);
        if (!this.root) { this.root = node; return this.root; }
        var current = this.root;
        while (true) {
            if (key < current.key) {
                if (!current.left) { current.left = node; break; }
                current = current.left;
            } else {
                if (!current.right) { current.right = node; break; }
                current = current.right;
            }
        }
        this.rebalance(current);
        return node;
    };
    this.rebalance = function(node) {
        node.height = Math.max(this.height(node.left), this.height(node.right)) + 1;
        var balance = this.balanceFactor(node);
        if (balance > 1) {
            if (this.balanceFactor(node.left) < 0) node.left = this.rotateLeft(node.left);
            return this.rotateRight(node);
        }
        if (balance < -1) {
            if (this.balanceFactor(node.right) > 0) node.right = this.rotateRight(node.right);
            return this.rotateLeft(node);
        }
        return node;
    };
    this.search = function(key) {
        var current = this.root;
        while (current) {
            if (key === current.key) return current.value;
            if (key < current.key) current = current.left;
            else current = current.right;
        }
        return null;
    };
    this.inOrder = function(node, result) {
        result = result || [];
        if (node) {
            this.inOrder(node.left, result);
            result.push({ key: node.key, value: node.value });
            this.inOrder(node.right, result);
        }
        return result;
    };
    this.preOrder = function(node, result) {
        result = result || [];
        if (node) {
            result.push({ key: node.key, value: node.value });
            this.preOrder(node.left, result);
            this.preOrder(node.right, result);
        }
        return result;
    };
    this.postOrder = function(node, result) {
        result = result || [];
        if (node) {
            this.postOrder(node.left, result);
            this.postOrder(node.right, result);
            result.push({ key: node.key, value: node.value });
        }
        return result;
    };
};
var RedBlackTree = function() {
    this.RED = true;
    this.BLACK = false;
    this.root = null;
    this.Node = function(key, value) {
        this.key = key;
        this.value = value;
        this.color = this.RED;
        this.left = null;
        this.right = null;
        this.parent = null;
    };
    this.rotateLeft = function(node) {
        var right = node.right;
        node.right = right.left;
        if (right.left) right.left.parent = node;
        right.parent = node.parent;
        if (!node.parent) this.root = right;
        else if (node === node.parent.left) node.parent.left = right;
        else node.parent.right = right;
        right.left = node;
        node.parent = right;
    };
    this.rotateRight = function(node) {
        var left = node.left;
        node.left = left.right;
        if (left.right) left.right.parent = node;
        left.parent = node.parent;
        if (!node.parent) this.root = left;
        else if (node === node.parent.right) node.parent.right = left;
        else node.parent.left = left;
        left.right = node;
        node.parent = left;
    };
    this.insert = function(key, value) {
        var node = new this.Node(key, value);
        var parent = null;
        var current = this.root;
        while (current) {
            parent = current;
            if (node.key < current.key) current = current.left;
            else current = current.right;
        }
        node.parent = parent;
        if (!parent) this.root = node;
        else if (node.key < parent.key) parent.left = node;
        else parent.right = node;
        this.insertFixup(node);
    };
    this.insertFixup = function(node) {
        while (node.parent && node.parent.color === this.RED) {
            if (node.parent === node.parent.parent.left) {
                var uncle = node.parent.parent.right;
                if (uncle && uncle.color === this.RED) {
                    node.parent.color = this.BLACK;
                    uncle.color = this.BLACK;
                    node.parent.parent.color = this.RED;
                    node = node.parent.parent;
                } else {
                    if (node === node.parent.right) { node = node.parent; this.rotateLeft(node); }
                    node.parent.color = this.BLACK;
                    node.parent.parent.color = this.RED;
                    this.rotateRight(node.parent.parent);
                }
            } else {
                var uncle = node.parent.parent.left;
                if (uncle && uncle.color === this.RED) {
                    node.parent.color = this.BLACK;
                    uncle.color = this.BLACK;
                    node.parent.parent.color = this.RED;
                    node = node.parent.parent;
                } else {
                    if (node === node.parent.left) { node = node.parent; this.rotateRight(node); }
                    node.parent.color = this.BLACK;
                    node.parent.parent.color = this.RED;
                    this.rotateLeft(node.parent.parent);
                }
            }
        }
        this.root.color = this.BLACK;
    };
    this.search = function(key) {
        var current = this.root;
        while (current) {
            if (key === current.key) return current.value;
            if (key < current.key) current = current.left;
            else current = current.right;
        }
        return null;
    };
};
var BTree = function(order) {
    this.order = order || 3;
    this.root = null;
    this.Node = function() {
        this.keys = [];
        this.values = [];
        this.children = [];
        this.leaf = true;
        this.n = 0;
    };
    this.insert = function(key, value) {
        if (!this.root) this.root = new this.Node();
        if (this.root.n === 2 * this.order - 1) {
            var newRoot = new this.Node();
            newRoot.leaf = false;
            newRoot.children.push(this.root);
            this.splitChild(newRoot, 0);
            this.root = newRoot;
        }
        this.insertNonFull(this.root, key, value);
    };
    this.splitChild = function(parent, i) {
        var child = parent.children[i];
        var newChild = new this.Node();
        newChild.leaf = child.leaf;
        for (var j = 0; j < this.order - 1; j++) {
            newChild.keys.push(child.keys[j + this.order]);
            newChild.values.push(child.values[j + this.order]);
        }
        if (!child.leaf) {
            for (var j = 0; j < this.order; j++) newChild.children.push(child.children[j + this.order]);
        }
        child.n = this.order - 1;
        for (var j = parent.n; j > i; j--) parent.children[j + 1] = parent.children[j];
        parent.children[i + 1] = newChild;
        for (var j = parent.n - 1; j >= i; j--) {
            parent.keys[j + 1] = parent.keys[j];
            parent.values[j + 1] = parent.values[j];
        }
        parent.keys[i] = child.keys[this.order - 1];
        parent.values[i] = child.values[this.order - 1];
        parent.n++;
    };
    this.insertNonFull = function(node, key, value) {
        var i = node.n - 1;
        if (node.leaf) {
            while (i >= 0 && key < node.keys[i]) i--;
            i++;
            node.keys.splice(i, 0, key);
            node.values.splice(i, 0, value);
            node.n++;
        } else {
            while (i >= 0 && key < node.keys[i]) i--;
            i++;
            if (node.children[i].n === 2 * this.order - 1) {
                this.splitChild(node, i);
                if (key > node.keys[i]) i++;
            }
            this.insertNonFull(node.children[i], key, value);
        }
    };
    this.search = function(key) {
        return this.searchNode(this.root, key);
    };
    this.searchNode = function(node, key) {
        var i = 0;
        while (i < node.n && key > node.keys[i]) i++;
        if (i < node.n && key === node.keys[i]) return node.values[i];
        if (node.leaf) return null;
        return this.searchNode(node.children[i], key);
    };
};
var SkipListNode = function(key, value) {
    this.key = key;
    this.value = value;
    this.forward = [];
};
var SkipList = function(maxLevel, probability) {
    this.maxLevel = maxLevel || 16;
    this.probability = probability || 0.5;
    this.header = new SkipListNode(null, null);
    this.level = 0;
    this.randomLevel = function() {
        var level = 0;
        while (Math.random() < this.probability && level < this.maxLevel) level++;
        return level;
    };
    this.insert = function(key, value) {
        var update = [];
        var current = this.header;
        for (var i = this.level; i >= 0; i--) {
            while (current.forward[i] && current.forward[i].key < key) current = current.forward[i];
            update[i] = current;
        }
        current = current.forward[0];
        if (current && current.key === key) { current.value = value; return; }
        var newLevel = this.randomLevel();
        if (newLevel > this.level) {
            for (var i = this.level + 1; i <= newLevel; i++) update[i] = this.header;
            this.level = newLevel;
        }
        var newNode = new SkipListNode(key, value);
        for (var i = 0; i <= newLevel; i++) {
            newNode.forward[i] = update[i].forward[i];
            update[i].forward[i] = newNode;
        }
    };
    this.search = function(key) {
        var current = this.header;
        for (var i = this.level; i >= 0; i--) {
            while (current.forward[i] && current.forward[i].key < key) current = current.forward[i];
        }
        current = current.forward[0];
        if (current && current.key === key) return current.value;
        return null;
    };
    this.remove = function(key) {
        var update = [];
        var current = this.header;
        for (var i = this.level; i >= 0; i--) {
            while (current.forward[i] && current.forward[i].key < key) current = current.forward[i];
            update[i] = current;
        }
        current = current.forward[0];
        if (!current || current.key !== key) return;
        for (var i = 0; i <= this.level; i++) {
            if (update[i].forward[i] !== current) break;
            update[i].forward[i] = current.forward[i];
        }
        while (this.level > 0 && !this.header.forward[this.level]) this.level--;
    };
};
var CurveFitting = function() {
    this.linearRegression = function(points) {
        var n = points.length;
        var sumX = 0, sumY = 0, sumXY = 0, sumX2 = 0;
        for (var i = 0; i < n; i++) {
            sumX += points[i].x;
            sumY += points[i].y;
            sumXY += points[i].x * points[i].y;
            sumX2 += points[i].x * points[i].x;
        }
        var slope = (n * sumXY - sumX * sumY) / (n * sumX2 - sumX * sumX);
        var intercept = (sumY - slope * sumX) / n;
        return { slope: slope, intercept: intercept, fn: function(x) { return slope * x + intercept; } };
    };
    this.polynomialRegression = function(points, degree) {
        var n = points.length;
        var x = [], y = [];
        for (var i = 0; i < n; i++) { x.push(points[i].x); y.push(points[i].y); }
        var coeffs = this.solvePolynomial(x, y, degree);
        return {
            coeffs: coeffs,
            fn: function(x) {
                var result = 0;
                for (var i = 0; i <= degree; i++) result += coeffs[i] * Math.pow(x, i);
                return result;
            }
        };
    };
    this.solvePolynomial = function(x, y, degree) {
        var n = x.length;
        var matrix = [];
        for (var i = 0; i <= degree; i++) {
            matrix.push([]);
            for (var j = 0; j <= degree; j++) {
                var sum = 0;
                for (var k = 0; k < n; k++) sum += Math.pow(x[k], i + j);
                matrix[i].push(sum);
            }
            var sum = 0;
            for (var k = 0; k < n; k++) sum += y[k] * Math.pow(x[k], i);
            matrix[i].push(sum);
        }
        return this.gaussianElimination(matrix);
    };
    this.gaussianElimination = function(matrix) {
        var n = matrix.length;
        var coeffs = new Array(n).fill(0);
        for (var i = 0; i < n; i++) {
            var maxRow = i;
            for (var k = i + 1; k < n; k++) if (Math.abs(matrix[k][i]) > Math.abs(matrix[maxRow][i])) maxRow = k;
            var temp = matrix[i];
            matrix[i] = matrix[maxRow];
            matrix[maxRow] = temp;
            for (var k = i + 1; k < n; k++) {
                var factor = matrix[k][i] / matrix[i][i];
                for (var j = i; j <= n; j++) matrix[k][j] -= factor * matrix[i][j];
            }
        }
        for (var i = n - 1; i >= 0; i--) {
            coeffs[i] = matrix[i][n];
            for (var j = i + 1; j < n; j++) coeffs[i] -= matrix[i][j] * coeffs[j];
            coeffs[i] /= matrix[i][i];
        }
        return coeffs;
    };
    this.exponentialRegression = function(points) {
        var n = points.length;
        var sumX = 0, sumY = 0, sumXY = 0, sumX2 = 0;
        var yLog = [];
        for (var i = 0; i < n; i++) {
            if (points[i].y <= 0) return null;
            yLog.push(Math.log(points[i].y));
            sumX += points[i].x;
            sumY += yLog[i];
            sumXY += points[i].x * yLog[i];
            sumX2 += points[i].x * points[i].x;
        }
        var slope = (n * sumXY - sumX * sumY) / (n * sumX2 - sumX * sumX);
        var intercept = (sumY - slope * sumX) / n;
        var a = Math.exp(intercept);
        var b = slope;
        return { a: a, b: b, fn: function(x) { return a * Math.exp(b * x); } };
    };
    this.logarithmicRegression = function(points) {
        var n = points.length;
        var sumX = 0, sumY = 0, sumXLogY = 0, sumX2 = 0;
        for (var i = 0; i < n; i++) {
            if (points[i].x <= 0) return null;
            var logX = Math.log(points[i].x);
            sumX += logX;
            sumY += points[i].y;
            sumXLogY += logX * points[i].y;
            sumX2 += logX * logX;
        }
        var slope = (n * sumXLogY - sumX * sumY) / (n * sumX2 - sumX * sumX);
        var intercept = (sumY - slope * sumX) / n;
