        return { slope: slope, intercept: intercept, fn: function(x) { return slope * Math.log(x) + intercept; } };
    };
};
var HuffmanCoding = function() {
    this.freqMap = {};
    this.tree = null;
    this.codes = {};
    this.buildFrequencyMap = function(data) {
        this.freqMap = {};
        for (var i = 0; i < data.length; i++) {
            var char = data[i];
            this.freqMap[char] = (this.freqMap[char] || 0) + 1;
        }
    };
    this.buildTree = function() {
        var nodes = [];
        for (var char in this.freqMap) {
            nodes.push({ char: char, freq: this.freqMap[char], left: null, right: null });
        }
        while (nodes.length > 1) {
            nodes.sort(function(a, b) { return a.freq - b.freq; });
            var left = nodes.shift();
            var right = nodes.shift();
            var parent = { char: null, freq: left.freq + right.freq, left: left, right: right };
            nodes.push(parent);
        }
        this.tree = nodes[0];
    };
    this.buildCodes = function(node, code) {
        code = code || "";
        if (node.char) {
            this.codes[node.char] = code || "0";
            return;
        }
        this.buildCodes(node.left, code + "0");
        this.buildCodes(node.right, code + "1");
    };
    this.encode = function(data) {
        this.buildFrequencyMap(data);
        this.buildTree();
        this.codes = {};
        this.buildCodes(this.tree);
        var encoded = "";
        for (var i = 0; i < data.length; i++) {
            encoded += this.codes[data[i]];
        }
        return { encoded: encoded, codes: this.codes, tree: this.tree };
    };
    this.decode = function(encodedData, tree) {
        var decoded = "";
        var current = tree;
        for (var i = 0; i < encodedData.length; i++) {
            if (encodedData[i] === "0") current = current.left;
            else current = current.right;
            if (current.char) {
                decoded += current.char;
                current = tree;
            }
        }
        return decoded;
    };
};
var LZWCoding = function() {
    this.dictionary = {};
    this.dictSize = 256;
    this.initializeDictionary = function() {
        this.dictionary = {};
        this.dictSize = 256;
        for (var i = 0; i < 256; i++) this.dictionary[String.fromCharCode(i)] = i;
    };
    this.encode = function(data) {
        this.initializeDictionary();
        var result = [];
        var w = "";
        for (var i = 0; i < data.length; i++) {
            var c = data[i];
            var wc = w + c;
            if (this.dictionary.hasOwnProperty(wc)) w = wc;
            else {
                result.push(this.dictionary[w]);
                this.dictionary[wc] = this.dictSize++;
                w = c;
            }
        }
        if (w) result.push(this.dictionary[w]);
        return result;
    };
    this.decode = function(compressed) {
        this.initializeDictionary();
        var w = String.fromCharCode(compressed[0]);
        var result = w;
        for (var i = 1; i < compressed.length; i++) {
            var k = compressed[i];
            var entry = (k < this.dictSize) ? this.getEntry(k) : String.fromCharCode(k) + w;
            result += entry;
            this.dictionary[this.dictSize++] = w + entry[0];
            w = entry;
        }
        return result;
    };
    this.getEntry = function(k) {
        for (var key in this.dictionary) {
            if (this.dictionary[key] === k) return key;
        }
        return String.fromCharCode(k);
    };
};
var RunLengthEncoding = function() {
    this.encode = function(data) {
        if (!data || data.length === 0) return "";
        var result = "";
        var count = 1;
        for (var i = 1; i < data.length; i++) {
            if (data[i] === data[i - 1]) count++;
            else {
                result += count + data[i - 1];
                count = 1;
            }
        }
        result += count + data[data.length - 1];
        return result;
    };
    this.decode = function(encoded) {
        if (!encoded || encoded.length === 0) return "";
        var result = "";
        var i = 0;
        while (i < encoded.length) {
            var numStr = "";
            while (i < encoded.length && encoded[i] >= "0" && encoded[i] <= "9") {
                numStr += encoded[i++];
            }
            var count = parseInt(numStr) || 1;
            if (i < encoded.length) {
                result += encoded[i].repeat(count);
                i++;
            }
        }
        return result;
    };
};
var BaseConvert = function() {
    this.toBase = function(num, base, digits) {
        digits = digits || "0123456789ABCDEFGHIJKLMNOPQRSTUVWXYZabcdefghijklmnopqrstuvwxyz+/";
        if (base < 2 || base > digits.length) return null;
        var result = "";
        var negative = num < 0;
        num = Math.abs(num);
        while (num > 0) {
            result = digits[num % base] + result;
            num = Math.floor(num / base);
        }
        return negative ? "-" + result : result;
    };
    this.fromBase = function(str, base, digits) {
        digits = digits || "0123456789ABCDEFGHIJKLMNOPQRSTUVWXYZabcdefghijklmnopqrstuvwxyz+/";
        if (base < 2 || base > digits.length) return null;
        var result = 0;
        var negative = str[0] === "-";
        str = negative ? str.substring(1) : str;
        for (var i = 0; i < str.length; i++) {
            var digit = digits.indexOf(str[i]);
            if (digit < 0 || digit >= base) return null;
            result = result * base + digit;
        }
        return negative ? -result : result;
    };
    this.toBinary = function(num) { return this.toBase(num, 2); };
    this.fromBinary = function(str) { return this.fromBase(str, 2); };
    this.toHex = function(num) { return this.toBase(num, 16); };
    this.fromHex = function(str) { return this.fromBase(str, 16); };
    this.toOctal = function(num) { return this.toBase(num, 8); };
    this.fromOctal = function(str) { return this.fromBase(str, 8); };
};
var RomanNumerals = function() {
    this.toRoman = function(num) {
        if (num <= 0 || num > 3999) return "";
        var vals = [1000, 900, 500, 400, 100, 90, 50, 40, 10, 9, 5, 4, 1];
        var syms = ["M", "CM", "D", "CD", "C", "XC", "L", "XL", "X", "IX", "V", "IV", "I"];
        var result = "";
        for (var i = 0; i < vals.length; i++) {
            while (num >= vals[i]) {
                result += syms[i];
                num -= vals[i];
            }
        }
        return result;
    };
    this.fromRoman = function(roman) {
        if (!roman) return 0;
        var vals = { I: 1, V: 5, X: 10, L: 50, C: 100, D: 500, M: 1000 };
        var result = 0;
        var prev = 0;
        for (var i = roman.length - 1; i >= 0; i--) {
            var curr = vals[roman[i]];
            if (curr < prev) result -= curr;
            else result += curr;
            prev = curr;
        }
        return result;
    };
};
var WordWrap = function() {
    this.wrap = function(text, width) {
        if (!text) return "";
        var words = text.split(" ");
        var lines = [];
        var currentLine = "";
        for (var i = 0; i < words.length; i++) {
            var word = words[i];
            if ((currentLine + " " + word).trim().length <= width) {
                currentLine = (currentLine + " " + word).trim();
            } else {
                if (currentLine) lines.push(currentLine);
                currentLine = word;
            }
        }
        if (currentLine) lines.push(currentLine);
        return lines.join("\n");
    };
    this.wrapHard = function(text, width) {
        if (!text) return "";
        var result = "";
        for (var i = 0; i < text.length; i++) {
            result += text[i];
            if ((i + 1) % width === 0) result += "\n";
        }
        return result;
    };
    this.wrapOptimal = function(text, width) {
        if (!text || width <= 0) return "";
        var words = text.split(/\s+/);
        var lines = [];
        var currentLine = [];
        var currentLength = 0;
        for (var i = 0; i < words.length; i++) {
            var word = words[i];
            if (currentLength + word.length + (currentLine.length > 0 ? 1 : 0) <= width) {
                currentLine.push(word);
                currentLength += word.length + (currentLine.length > 1 ? 1 : 0);
            } else {
                if (currentLine.length > 0) lines.push(currentLine.join(" "));
                currentLine = [word];
                currentLength = word.length;
            }
        }
        if (currentLine.length > 0) lines.push(currentLine.join(" "));
        return lines.join("\n");
    };
};
var Soundex = function() {
    this.codes = { B: 1, F: 1, P: 1, V: 1, C: 2, G: 2, J: 2, K: 2, Q: 2, S: 2, X: 2, Z: 2, D: 3, T: 3, L: 4, M: 5, N: 5, R: 6 };
    this.encode = function(word) {
        if (!word || word.length === 0) return "";
        word = word.toUpperCase();
        var first = word[0];
        var result = first;
        var prevCode = this.codes[first] || 0;
        for (var i = 1; i < word.length && result.length < 4; i++) {
            var code = this.codes[word[i]] || 0;
            if (code !== 0 && code !== prevCode) {
                result += code;
            }
            if (code !== 0) prevCode = code;
        }
        return (result + "000").substring(0, 4);
    };
    this.similarity = function(word1, word2) {
        return this.encode(word1) === this.encode(word2);
    };
};
var Metaphone = function() {
    this.encode = function(word) {
        if (!word || word.length === 0) return "";
        word = word.toUpperCase();
        if (word.substring(0, 2) === "KN" || word.substring(0, 2) === "AE" || word.substring(0, 2) === "WR") word = word.substring(1);
        if (word.substring(0, 2) === "WH") word = "W" + word.substring(2);
        var result = "";
        var prev = "";
        for (var i = 0; i < word.length && result.length < 4; i++) {
            var c = word[i];
            var isVowel = "AEIOU".indexOf(c) >= 0;
            if (isVowel && result.length === 0) result += c;
            else if ("B" === c && prev !== "M") result += c;
            else if ("C" === c && "IEY".indexOf(word[i + 1]) < 0) result += "K";
            else if ("D" === c && "IEY".indexOf(word[i + 1]) >= 0) result += "J";
            else if ("G" === c && "IEY".indexOf(word[i + 1]) < 0) result += "K";
            else if ("H" === c && "AEIOU".indexOf(word[i + 1]) >= 0 && prev !== "C") result += "H";
            else if ("K" === c && prev !== "C") result += "K";
            else if ("P" === c && word[i + 1] !== "H") result += "P";
            else if ("Q" === c) result += "K";
            else if ("S" === c && word.substring(i + 1, i + 2) === "HI") result += "X";
            else if ("T" === c && word.substring(i + 1, i + 2) === "HI") result += "X";
            else if ("V" === c) result += "F";
            else if ("W" === c && "AEIOU".indexOf(word[i + 1]) < 0) result += "";
            else if ("X" === c) result += "KS";
            else if ("Y" === c && "AEIOU".indexOf(word[i + 1]) < 0) result += "";
            else if ("Z" === c) result += "S";
            else if ("F" === c || "L" === c || "M" === c || "N" === c || "R" === c) result += c;
            prev = c;
        }
        return result.substring(0, 4);
    };
};
var LevenshteinDistance = function() {
    this.distance = function(s1, s2) {
        if (!s1 && !s2) return 0;
        if (!s1) return s2.length;
        if (!s2) return s1.length;
        var m = s1.length, n = s2.length;
        var d = [];
        for (var i = 0; i <= m; i++) d.push([i]);
        for (var j = 0; j <= n; j++) d[0].push(j);
        for (var i = 1; i <= m; i++) {
            for (var j = 1; j <= n; j++) {
                var cost = (s1[i - 1] === s2[j - 1]) ? 0 : 1;
                d[i][j] = Math.min(d[i - 1][j] + 1, d[i][j - 1] + 1, d[i - 1][j - 1] + cost);
            }
        }
        return d[m][n];
    };
    this.similarity = function(s1, s2) {
        var maxLen = Math.max(s1.length, s2.length);
        if (maxLen === 0) return 1;
        return 1 - this.distance(s1, s2) / maxLen;
    };
};
var HammingDistance = function() {
    this.distance = function(s1, s2) {
        if (s1.length !== s2.length) return -1;
        var dist = 0;
        for (var i = 0; i < s1.length; i++) {
            if (s1[i] !== s2[i]) dist++;
        }
        return dist;
    };
    this.similarity = function(s1, s2) {
        if (s1.length !== s2.length) return 0;
        var matches = 0;
        for (var i = 0; i < s1.length; i++) {
            if (s1[i] === s2[i]) matches++;
        }
        return matches / s1.length;
    };
};
var JaroWinkler = function() {
    this.similarity = function(s1, s2) {
        if (!s1 || !s2) return 0;
        if (s1 === s2) return 1;
        var len1 = s1.length, len2 = s2.length;
        var matchWindow = Math.floor(Math.max(len1, len2) / 2) - 1;
        var matches1 = new Array(len1).fill(false);
        var matches2 = new Array(len2).fill(false);
        var matches = 0;
        var transpositions = 0;
        for (var i = 0; i < len1; i++) {
            var start = Math.max(0, i - matchWindow);
            var end = Math.min(i + matchWindow + 1, len2);
            for (var j = start; j < end; j++) {
                if (matches2[j] || s1[i] !== s2[j]) continue;
                matches1[i] = true;
                matches2[j] = true;
                matches++;
                break;
            }
        }
        if (matches === 0) return 0;
        var k = 0;
        for (var i = 0; i < len1; i++) {
            if (!matches1[i]) continue;
            while (!matches2[k]) k++;
            if (s1[i] !== s2[k]) transpositions++;
            k++;
        }
        var jaro = (matches / len1 + matches / len2 + (matches - transpositions / 2) / matches) / 3;
        var prefix = 0;
        for (var i = 0; i < Math.min(4, Math.min(len1, len2)); i++) {
            if (s1[i] === s2[i]) prefix++;
            else break;
        }
        return jaro + prefix * 0.1 * (1 - jaro);
    };
};
var DiceCoefficient = function() {
    this.coefficient = function(s1, s2) {
        if (!s1 || !s2) return 0;
        if (s1 === s2) return 1;
        var bigrams1 = this.getBigrams(s1);
        var bigrams2 = this.getBigrams(s2);
        var intersection = 0;
        for (var i = 0; i < bigrams1.length; i++) {
            for (var j = 0; j < bigrams2.length; j++) {
                if (bigrams1[i] === bigrams2[j]) {
                    intersection++;
                    bigrams2[j] = "";
                    break;
                }
            }
        }
        return 2 * intersection / (bigrams1.length + bigrams2.length);
    };
    this.getBigrams = function(s) {
        var bigrams = [];
        for (var i = 0; i < s.length - 1; i++) bigrams.push(s.substring(i, i + 2));
        return bigrams;
    };
};
var PorterStemmer = function() {
    this.stem = function(word) {
        word = word.toLowerCase();
        if (word.length <= 2) return word;
        word = this.step1a(word);
        word = this.step1b(word);
        word = this.step1c(word);
        word = this.step2(word);
        word = this.step3(word);
        word = this.step4(word);
        word = this.step5a(word);
        word = this.step5b(word);
        return word;
    };
    this.step1a = function(word) {
        if (word.endsWith("sses")) return word.substring(0, word.length - 2);
        if (word.endsWith("ies")) return word.substring(0, word.length - 2);
        if (word.endsWith("ss")) return word;
        return word.substring(0, word.length - 1);
    };
    this.step1b = function(word) {
        if (word.endsWith("eed")) {
            if (this.getM(word.substring(0, word.length - 3)) > 0) return word.substring(0, word.length - 1);
            return word;
        }
        var vowelStem = word.match(/[aeiou]/);
        var ed = word.endsWith("ed");
        var ing = word.endsWith("ing");
        if (ed || ing) {
            var test = ed ? word.substring(0, word.length - 2) : word.substring(0, word.length - 3);
            if (vowelStem && test.match(/[aeiou]/)) {
                word = test;
                if (word.endsWith("at") || word.endsWith("bl") || word.endsWith("iz")) return word + "e";
                if (this.doubleConsonant(word) && !word.endsWith("l") && !word.endsWith("s") && !word.endsWith("z")) return word.substring(0, word.length - 1);
                if (this.getM(word) === 1 && this.cvc(word)) return word + "e";
            }
        }
        return word;
    };
    this.step1c = function(word) {
        if (word.endsWith("y")) {
            var base = word.substring(0, word.length - 1);
            if (base.match(/[aeiou]/)) return base + "i";
        }
        return word;
    };
    this.step2 = function(word) {
        var suffixes = ["ational", "tional", "enci", "anci", "izer", "abli", "alli", "entli", "eli", "ousli", "ization", "ation", "ator", "alism", "iveness", "fulness", "ousness", "aliti", "iviti", "biliti", "logi"];
        for (var i = 0; i < suffixes.length; i++) {
            if (word.endsWith(suffixes[i]) && this.getM(word.substring(0, word.length - suffixes[i].length)) > 0) {
                return word.substring(0, word.length - suffixes[i].length) + (suffixes[i].endsWith("i") ? "ize" : suffixes[i].substring(0, suffixes[i].length - 2));
            }
        }
        return word;
    };
    this.step3 = function(word) {
        var suffixes = ["icate", "ative", "alize", "iciti", "ical", "ful", "ness"];
        for (var i = 0; i < suffixes.length; i++) {
            if (word.endsWith(suffixes[i]) && this.getM(word.substring(0, word.length - suffixes[i].length)) > 0) {
                return word.substring(0, word.length - suffixes[i].length);
            }
        }
        return word;
    };
    this.step4 = function(word) {
        var suffixes = ["al", "ance", "ence", "er", "ic", "able", "ible", "ant", "ement", "ment", "ent", "ion", "ou", "ism", "ate", "iti", "ous", "ive", "ize"];
        for (var i = 0; i < suffixes.length; i++) {
            if (word.endsWith(suffixes[i])) {
                var base = word.substring(0, word.length - suffixes[i].length);
                if (this.getM(base) > 1) return base;
            }
        }
        return word;
    };
    this.step5a = function(word) {
        if (word.endsWith("e") && this.getM(word.substring(0, word.length - 1)) > 1) {
            if (this.cvc(word.substring(0, word.length - 1))) return word;
            return word.substring(0, word.length - 1);
        }
        return word;
    };
    this.step5b = function(word) {
        if (this.getM(word) > 1 && this.doubleConsonant(word) && word.endsWith("l")) {
            return word.substring(0, word.length - 1);
        }
        return word;
    };
    this.getM = function(word) {
        var cvPattern = /[aeiou]+[^aeiou]+/g;
        var matches = word.match(cvPattern);
        return matches ? matches.length : 0;
    };
    this.doubleConsonant = function(word) {
        if (word.length < 3) return false;
        return word[word.length - 1] === word[word.length - 2] && !"aeiou".includes(word[word.length - 1]);
    };
    this.cvc = function(word) {
        if (word.length < 3) return false;
        return !"aeiou".includes(word[word.length - 1]) && word[word.length - 1] !== "w" && word[word.length - 1] !== "x" && word[word.length - 1] !== "y" && "aeiou".includes(word[word.length - 3]);
    };
};
var NaiveBayesClassifier = function() {
    this.classes = {};
    this.vocabulary = {};
    this.totalDocuments = 0;
    this.tokenize = function(text) {
        return text.toLowerCase().split(/\s+/);
    };
    this.train = function(document, classLabel) {
        if (!this.classes[classLabel]) {
            this.classes[classLabel] = { documents: 0, wordCounts: {}, wordTotal: 0 };
        }
        this.classes[classLabel].documents++;
        this.totalDocuments++;
        var tokens = this.tokenize(document);
        for (var i = 0; i < tokens.length; i++) {
            var word = tokens[i];
            this.vocabulary[word] = true;
            this.classes[classLabel].wordCounts[word] = (this.classes[classLabel].wordCounts[word] || 0) + 1;
            this.classes[classLabel].wordTotal++;
        }
    };
    this.classify = function(document) {
        var tokens = this.tokenize(document);
        var bestClass = null;
        var bestProb = -Infinity;
        for (var classLabel in this.classes) {
            var logProb = Math.log(this.classes[classLabel].documents / this.totalDocuments);
            for (var i = 0; i < tokens.length; i++) {
                var word = tokens[i];
                var wordCount = this.classes[classLabel].wordCounts[word] || 0;
                var vocabSize = Object.keys(this.vocabulary).length;
                var probability = (wordCount + 1) / (this.classes[classLabel].wordTotal + vocabSize);
                logProb += Math.log(probability);
            }
            if (logProb > bestProb) {
                bestProb = logProb;
                bestClass = classLabel;
            }
        }
        return bestClass;
    };
};
var KMeansClustering = function() {
    this.centroids = [];
    this.k = 3;
    this.maxIterations = 100;
    this.initialize = function(points, k) {
        this.k = k || this.k;
        this.centroids = [];
        var used = {};
        while (this.centroids.length < this.k) {
            var idx = Math.floor(Math.random() * points.length);
            if (!used[idx]) {
                used[idx] = true;
                this.centroids.push({ x: points[idx].x, y: points[idx].y });
            }
        }
    };
    this.assignClusters = function(points) {
        var clusters = [];
        for (var i = 0; i < this.k; i++) clusters.push([]);
        for (var i = 0; i < points.length; i++) {
            var minDist = Infinity;
            var cluster = 0;
            for (var j = 0; j < this.k; j++) {
                var dist = Math.pow(points[i].x - this.centroids[j].x, 2) + Math.pow(points[i].y - this.centroids[j].y, 2);
                if (dist < minDist) {
                    minDist = dist;
                    cluster = j;
                }
            }
            clusters[cluster].push(points[i]);
        }
        return clusters;
    };
    this.updateCentroids = function(clusters) {
        for (var i = 0; i < this.k; i++) {
            if (clusters[i].length === 0) continue;
            var sumX = 0, sumY = 0;
            for (var j = 0; j < clusters[i].length; j++) {
                sumX += clusters[i][j].x;
                sumY += clusters[i][j].y;
            }
            this.centroids[i].x = sumX / clusters[i].length;
            this.centroids[i].y = sumY / clusters[i].length;
        }
    };
    this.fit = function(points, k) {
        this.initialize(points, k);
        for (var iter = 0; iter < this.maxIterations; iter++) {
            var clusters = this.assignClusters(points);
            var prevCentroids = JSON.stringify(this.centroids);
            this.updateCentroids(clusters);
            if (JSON.stringify(this.centroids) === prevCentroids) break;
        }
        return { centroids: this.centroids, clusters: this.assignClusters(points) };
    };
};
var DecisionTreeClassifier = function() {
    this.root = null;
    this.impurity = function(labels) {
        if (labels.length === 0) return 0;
        var counts = {};
        for (var i = 0; i < labels.length; i++) counts[labels[i]] = (counts[labels[i]] || 0) + 1;
        var impurity = 1;
        for (var label in counts) {
            var prob = counts[label] / labels.length;
            impurity -= prob * prob;
        }
        return impurity;
    };
    this.bestSplit = function(data, featureIndex) {
        var left = [], right = [];
        var leftLabels = [], rightLabels = [];
        var threshold = data[0].features[featureIndex];
        for (var i = 0; i < data.length; i++) {
            if (data[i].features[featureIndex] <= threshold) {
                left.push(data[i]);
                leftLabels.push(data[i].label);
            } else {
                right.push(data[i]);
                rightLabels.push(data[i].label);
            }
        }
        var totalImpurity = this.impurity(leftLabels) + this.impurity(rightLabels);
        return { threshold: threshold, left: left, right: right, impurity: totalImpurity };
    };
    this.grow = function(data) {
        var labels = data.map(function(d) { return d.label; });
        if (this.impurity(labels) === 0) return { type: "leaf", label: labels[0] };
        var bestFeature = 0, bestSplit = null, bestImpurity = Infinity;
        for (var i = 0; i < data[0].features.length; i++) {
            var split = this.bestSplit(data, i);
            if (split.impurity < bestImpurity) {
                bestImpurity = split.impurity;
                bestSplit = split;
                bestFeature = i;
            }
        }
        if (bestImpurity === Infinity) return { type: "leaf", label: labels[0] };
        var left = this.grow(bestSplit.left);
        var right = this.grow(bestSplit.right);
        return { type: "split", feature: bestFeature, threshold: bestSplit.threshold, left: left, right: right };
    };
    this.train = function(data) {
        this.root = this.grow(data);
    };
    this.predictOne = function(sample, node) {
        if (node.type === "leaf") return node.label;
        if (sample.features[node.feature] <= node.threshold) return this.predictOne(sample, node.left);
        return this.predictOne(sample, node.right);
    };
    this.predict = function(sample) {
        return this.predictOne(sample, this.root);
    };
};
var SimpleMarkdown = function() {
    this.headings = function(text) {
        text = text.replace(/^### (.+)$/gm, "<h3>$1</h3>");
        text = text.replace(/^## (.+)$/gm, "<h2>$1</h2>");
        text = text.replace(/^# (.+)$/gm, "<h1>$1</h1>");
        return text;
    };
    this.bold = function(text) { return text.replace(/\*\*(.+?)\*\*/g, "<strong>$1</strong>"); };
    this.italic = function(text) { return text.replace(/\*(.+?)\*/g, "<em>$1</em>"); };
    this.code = function(text) { return text.replace(/`(.+?)`/g, "<code>$1</code>"); };
    this.links = function(text) { return text.replace(/\[(.+?)\]\((.+?)\)/g, "<a href=\"$2\">$1</a>"); };
    this.images = function(text) { return text.replace(/!\[(.+?)\]\((.+?)\)/g, "<img src=\"$2\" alt=\"$1\">"); };
    this.blockquotes = function(text) { return text.replace(/^> (.+)$/gm, "<blockquote>$1</blockquote>"); };
    this.unorderedLists = function(text) {
        var lines = text.split("\n");
        var inList = false;
        var result = [];
        for (var i = 0; i < lines.length; i++) {
            if (lines[i].match(/^- /)) {
                if (!inList) { result.push("<ul>"); inList = true; }
                result.push("<li>" + lines[i].substring(2) + "</li>");
            } else {
                if (inList) { result.push("</ul>"); inList = false; }
                result.push(lines[i]);
            }
        }
        if (inList) result.push("</ul>");
        return result.join("\n");
    };
    this.orderedLists = function(text) {
        var lines = text.split("\n");
        var inList = false;
        var result = [];
        for (var i = 0; i < lines.length; i++) {
            if (lines[i].match(/^\d+\. /)) {
                if (!inList) { result.push("<ol>"); inList = true; }
                result.push("<li>" + lines[i].replace(/^\d+\. /, "") + "</li>");
            } else {
                if (inList) { result.push("</ol>"); inList = false; }
                result.push(lines[i]);
            }
        }
        if (inList) result.push("</ol>");
        return result.join("\n");
    };
    this.horizontalRules = function(text) { return text.replace(/^---$/gm, "<hr>"); };
    this.paragraphs = function(text) {
        return text.replace(/\n\n/g, "</p><p>").replace(/^/, "<p>").replace(/$/, "</p>");
    };
    this.parse = function(md) {
        var html = md;
        html = this.headings(html);
        html = this.bold(html);
        html = this.italic(html);
        html = this.code(html);
        html = this.links(html);
        html = this.images(html);
        html = this.blockquotes(html);
        html = this.horizontalRules(html);
        html = this.unorderedLists(html);
        html = this.orderedLists(html);
        html = this.paragraphs(html);
        return html;
    };
};
var BBCodeParser = function() {
    this.parse = function(text) {
        text = text.replace(/\[b\](.+?)\[\/b\]/gi, "<strong>$1</strong>");
        text = text.replace(/\[i\](.+?)\[\/i\]/gi, "<em>$1</em>");
        text = text.replace(/\[u\](.+?)\[\/u\]/gi, "<u>$1</u>");
        text = text.replace(/\[s\](.+?)\[\/s\]/gi, "<s>$1</s>");
        text = text.replace(/\[url=(.+?)\](.+?)\[\/url\]/gi, "<a href=\"$1\">$2</a>");
        text = text.replace(/\[url\](.+?)\[\/url\]/gi, "<a href=\"$1\">$1</a>");
        text = text.replace(/\[img\](.+?)\[\/img\]/gi, "<img src=\"$1\">");
        text = text.replace(/\[color=(.+?)\](.+?)\[\/color\]/gi, "<span style=\"color:$1\">$2</span>");
        text = text.replace(/\[size=(.+?)\](.+?)\[\/size\]/gi, "<span style=\"font-size:$1px\">$2</span>");
        text = text.replace(/\[quote\](.+?)\[\/quote\]/gi, "<blockquote>$1</blockquote>");
        text = text.replace(/\[code\](.+?)\[\/code\]/gi, "<pre><code>$1</code></pre>");
        text = text.replace(/\[list\](.+?)\[\/list\]/gi, "<ul>$1</ul>");
        text = text.replace(/\[\*\](.+?)/g, "<li>$1</li>");
        return text;
    };
    this.toBBCode = function(html) {
        html = html.replace(/<strong>(.+?)<\/strong>/gi, "[b]$1[/b]");
        html = html.replace(/<em>(.+?)<\/em>/gi, "[i]$1[/i]");
        html = html.replace(/<u>(.+?)<\/u>/gi, "[u]$1[/u]");
        html = html.replace(/<s>(.+?)<\/s>/gi, "[s]$1[/s]");
        html = html.replace(/<a href="(.+?)">(.+?)<\/a>/gi, "[url=$1]$2[/url]");
        html = html.replace(/<img src="(.+?)">/gi, "[img]$1[/img]");
        return html;
    };
};
var URLQueryParser = function() {
    this.parse = function(query) {
        if (query.indexOf("?") === 0) query = query.substring(1);
        var params = {};
        if (!query) return params;
        var pairs = query.split("&");
        for (var i = 0; i < pairs.length; i++) {
            var kv = pairs[i].split("=");
            var key = decodeURIComponent(kv[0]);
            var value = kv.length > 1 ? decodeURIComponent(kv[1]) : "";
            if (params.hasOwnProperty(key)) {
                if (!(params[key] instanceof Array)) params[key] = [params[key]];
                params[key].push(value);
            } else {
                params[key] = value;
            }
        }
        return params;
    };
    this.build = function(params) {
        var parts = [];
        for (var key in params) {
            var value = params[key];
            if (value instanceof Array) {
                for (var i = 0; i < value.length; i++) {
                    parts.push(encodeURIComponent(key) + "=" + encodeURIComponent(value[i]));
                }
            } else {
                parts.push(encodeURIComponent(key) + "=" + encodeURIComponent(value));
            }
        }
        return parts.join("&");
    };
    this.get = function(query, key) {
        var params = this.parse(query);
        return params.hasOwnProperty(key) ? params[key] : null;
    };
    this.set = function(query, key, value) {
        var params = this.parse(query);
        params[key] = value;
        return "?" + this.build(params);
    };
    this.remove = function(query, key) {
        var params = this.parse(query);
        delete params[key];
        return "?" + this.build(params);
    };
};
var XMLSaxParser = function() {
    this.parse = function(xml, callbacks) {
        var currentElement = null;
        var currentText = "";
        var parser = new ActiveXObject("MSXML2.DOMDocument.6.0");
        parser.async = false;
        parser.loadXML(xml);
        var processNode = function(node) {
            if (node.nodeType === 1) {
                var attributes = {};
                if (node.attributes) {
                    for (var i = 0; i < node.attributes.length; i++) {
                        attributes[node.attributes.item(i).nodeName] = node.attributes.item(i).nodeValue;
                    }
                }
                if (callbacks.startElement) callbacks.startElement(node.nodeName, attributes);
                var children = node.childNodes;
                for (var i = 0; i < children.length; i++) {
                    if (children.item(i).nodeType === 3) {
                        if (callbacks.text) callbacks.text(children.item(i).nodeValue);
                    } else if (children.item(i).nodeType === 1) {
                        processNode(children.item(i));
                    }
                }
                if (callbacks.endElement) callbacks.endElement(node.nodeName);
            }
        };
        processNode(parser.documentElement);
    };
};
var JSONPath = function() {
    this.path = "";
    this.query = function(obj, path) {
        this.path = path;
        var parts = this.parsePath(path);
        return this.traverse(obj, parts);
    };
    this.parsePath = function(path) {
        var parts = path.replace(/\[(\d+)\]/g, ".$1").replace(/\.\[/g, "[").split(".");
        return parts.filter(function(p) { return p.length > 0; });
    };
    this.traverse = function(obj, parts) {
        if (parts.length === 0 || obj === null) return [obj];
        var part = parts[0];
        var remaining = parts.slice(1);
        if (part === "$") return this.traverse(obj, remaining);
        if (part === "*" || part === "") {
            if (obj instanceof Array) {
                var results = [];
                for (var i = 0; i < obj.length; i++) {
                    var subResults = this.traverse(obj[i], remaining);
                    for (var j = 0; j < subResults.length; j++) results.push(subResults[j]);
                }
                return results;
            } else if (typeof obj === "object") {
                var results = [];
                for (var key in obj) {
                    var subResults = this.traverse(obj[key], remaining);
                    for (var j = 0; j < subResults.length; j++) results.push(subResults[j]);
                }
                return results;
            }
            return [];
        }
        if (part.match(/^\d+$/)) {
            var index = parseInt(part);
            if (obj instanceof Array && index < obj.length) return this.traverse(obj[index], remaining);
            return [];
        }
        if (obj && obj.hasOwnProperty(part)) return this.traverse(obj[part], remaining);
        return [];
    };
};
var JSONPointer = function() {
    this.escape = function(str) {
        return str.replace(/~/g, "~0").replace(/\//g, "~1");
    };
    this.unescape = function(str) {
        return str.replace(/~1/g, "/").replace(/~0/g, "~");
    };
    this.parse = function(pointer) {
        if (!pointer || pointer === "/") return [];
        pointer = pointer.replace(/^\//, "");
        return pointer.split("/").map(this.unescape);
    };
    this.get = function(obj, pointer) {
        var tokens = this.parse(pointer);
        var current = obj;
        for (var i = 0; i < tokens.length; i++) {
            if (current === null || current === undefined) return undefined;
            current = current[tokens[i]];
        }
        return current;
    };
    this.set = function(obj, pointer, value) {
        var tokens = this.parse(pointer);
        var current = obj;
        for (var i = 0; i < tokens.length - 1; i++) {
            if (!(current[tokens[i]] instanceof Object)) current[tokens[i]] = {};
            current = current[tokens[i]];
        }
        current[tokens[tokens.length - 1]] = value;
        return obj;
    };
    this.remove = function(obj, pointer) {
        var tokens = this.parse(pointer);
        var current = obj;
        for (var i = 0; i < tokens.length - 1; i++) {
            if (current[tokens[i]] === undefined) return;
            current = current[tokens[i]];
        }
        delete current[tokens[tokens.length - 1]];
        return obj;
    };
};

var QueryStringBuilder = function() {
    this.params = {};
    this.set = function(key, value) {
        this.params[key] = value;
        return this;
    };
    this.append = function(key, value) {
        if (!this.params.hasOwnProperty(key)) this.params[key] = value;
        else if (this.params[key] instanceof Array) this.params[key].push(value);
        else this.params[key] = [this.params[key], value];
        return this;
    };
    this.get = function(key) { return this.params[key]; };
    this.has = function(key) { return this.params.hasOwnProperty(key); };
    this.remove = function(key) { delete this.params[key]; return this; };
    this.clear = function() { this.params = {}; return this; };
    this.toString = function() {
        var parts = [];
        for (var key in this.params) {
            var value = this.params[key];
            if (value instanceof Array) {
                for (var i = 0; i < value.length; i++) {
                    parts.push(encodeURIComponent(key) + "=" + encodeURIComponent(value[i]));
                }
            } else {
                parts.push(encodeURIComponent(key) + "=" + encodeURIComponent(value));
            }
        }
        return parts.join("&");
    };
    this.parse = function(query) {
        this.params = {};
        if (!query) return this;
        if (query.indexOf("?") === 0) query = query.substring(1);
        var pairs = query.split("&");
        for (var i = 0; i < pairs.length; i++) {
            var kv = pairs[i].split("=");
            var key = decodeURIComponent(kv[0]);
            var value = kv.length > 1 ? decodeURIComponent(kv[1]) : "";
            this.append(key, value);
        }
        return this;
    };
};
var URNParser = function() {
    this.parse = function(urn) {
        if (urn.indexOf("urn:") !== 0) return null;
        var parts = urn.substring(4).split(":");
        if (parts.length < 2) return null;
        var nid = parts[0];
        var nss = parts.slice(1).join(":");
        return { scheme: "urn", nid: nid, nss: nss };
    };
    this.build = function(nid, nss) {
        return "urn:" + nid + ":" + nss;
    };
    this.isValid = function(urn) {
        return /^urn:[a-zA-Z0-9][a-zA-Z0-9-]{0,31}:[a-zA-Z0-9()+,\-.:=@;$_!*'%\/?#]+$/.test(urn);
    };
};
var MediaQueryParser = function() {
    this.parse = function(query) {
        var result = { type: "all", conditions: [] };
        var parts = query.toLowerCase().split(" and ");
        if (parts[0].indexOf("(") < 0) result.type = parts[0].trim();
        for (var i = 0; i < parts.length; i++) {
            if (parts[i].indexOf("(") >= 0) {
                var match = parts[i].match(/([\w-]+)\s*:\s*(.+)/);
                if (match) {
                    var value = match[2].replace(/[()]/g, "").trim();
                    result.conditions.push({ property: match[1], value: value });
                }
            }
        }
        return result;
    };
    this.matches = function(query, viewport) {
        var parsed = this.parse(query);
        if (parsed.type !== "all" && parsed.type !== viewport.type) return false;
        for (var i = 0; i < parsed.conditions.length; i++) {
            var cond = parsed.conditions[i];
            if (cond.property === "min-width" && viewport.width < parseInt(cond.value)) return false;
            if (cond.property === "max-width" && viewport.width > parseInt(cond.value)) return false;
            if (cond.property === "min-height" && viewport.height < parseInt(cond.value)) return false;
            if (cond.property === "max-height" && viewport.height > parseInt(cond.value)) return false;
        }
        return true;
    };
};
var CSSSelectorParser = function() {
    this.parse = function(selector) {
        var parts = [];
        var current = "";
        for (var i = 0; i < selector.length; i++) {
            var c = selector[i];
            if (c === " ") {
                if (current) parts.push({ type: "descendant", value: current.trim() });
                current = "";
            } else if (c === ">") {
                if (current) parts.push({ type: "child", value: current.trim() });
                current = "";
            } else if (c === "+") {
                if (current) parts.push({ type: "adjacent", value: current.trim() });
                current = "";
            } else if (c === ".") {
                if (current) parts.push({ type: "class", value: current.trim() });
                current = "";
            } else if (c === "#") {
                if (current) parts.push({ type: "id", value: current.trim() });
                current = "";
            } else if (c === "[") {
                if (current) parts.push({ type: "tag", value: current.trim() });
                current = "";
                var close = selector.indexOf("]", i);
                if (close >= 0) {
                    var attr = selector.substring(i + 1, close);
                    parts.push({ type: "attribute", value: attr });
                    i = close;
                }
            } else {
                current += c;
            }
        }
        if (current) parts.push({ type: "tag", value: current.trim() });
        return parts;
    };
    this.getSpecificity = function(selector) {
        var parts = this.parse(selector);
        var ids = 0, classes = 0, tags = 0;
        for (var i = 0; i < parts.length; i++) {
            if (parts[i].type === "id") ids++;
            else if (parts[i].type === "class" || parts[i].type === "attribute") classes++;
            else if (parts[i].type === "tag") tags++;
        }
        return { ids: ids, classes: classes, tags: tags, total: ids * 100 + classes * 10 + tags };
    };
};
var CronParser = function() {
    this.parse = function(cronExpr) {
        var parts = cronExpr.split(" ");
        if (parts.length !== 5) return null;
        return {
            minute: parts[0],
            hour: parts[1],
            dayOfMonth: parts[2],
            month: parts[3],
            dayOfWeek: parts[4]
        };
    };
    this.isValid = function(cronExpr) {
        var parts = cronExpr.split(" ");
        if (parts.length !== 5) return false;
        var patterns = [/^(\*|([0-5]?\d))(,(\*|([0-5]?\d)))*$/, /^(\*|([01]?\d|2[0-3]))(,(\*|([01]?\d|2[0-3])))*$/,
                        /^(\*|([1-9]|[12]\d|3[01]))(,(\*|([1-9]|[12]\d|3[01])))*$/, /^(\*|([1-9]|1[0-2]))(,(\*|([1-9]|1[0-2])))*$/,
                        /^(\*|([0-6]))(,(\*|([0-6])))*$/];
        for (var i = 0; i < 5; i++) {
            if (!patterns[i].test(parts[i])) return false;
        }
        return true;
    };
    this.getNextRun = function(cronExpr, fromDate) {
        var schedule = this.parse(cronExpr);
        if (!schedule) return null;
        var date = fromDate ? new Date(fromDate) : new Date();
        date.setSeconds(0);
        date.setMilliseconds(0);
        date.setMinutes(date.getMinutes() + 1);
        return date;
    };
};
var INIParser = function() {
    this.parse = function(content) {
        var result = {};
        var currentSection = null;
        var lines = content.split(/\r?\n/);
        for (var i = 0; i < lines.length; i++) {
            var line = lines[i].trim();
            if (!line || line.charAt(0) === ";" || line.charAt(0) === "#") continue;
            if (line.charAt(0) === "[" && line.charAt(line.length - 1) === "]") {
                currentSection = line.substring(1, line.length - 1);
                result[currentSection] = {};
            } else if (currentSection) {
                var eqIndex = line.indexOf("=");
                if (eqIndex > 0) {
                    var key = line.substring(0, eqIndex).trim();
                    var value = line.substring(eqIndex + 1).trim();
                    result[currentSection][key] = value;
                }
            }
        }
        return result;
    };
    this.build = function(data) {
        var lines = [];
        for (var section in data) {
            lines.push("[" + section + "]");
            for (var key in data[section]) {
                lines.push(key + "=" + data[section][key]);
            }
            lines.push("");
        }
        return lines.join("\r\n");
    };
};
var YAMLParser = function() {
    this.parse = function(yaml) {
        var lines = yaml.split(/\r?\n/);
        var result = {};
        var stack = [{ obj: result, indent: -1 }];
        for (var i = 0; i < lines.length; i++) {
            var line = lines[i];
            if (!line.trim() || line.trim().charAt(0) === "#") continue;
            var indent = line.search(/\S/);
            while (stack.length > 1 && stack[stack.length - 1].indent >= indent) stack.pop();
            var trimmed = line.trim();
            if (trimmed.charAt(0) === "-" && trimmed.charAt(1) === " ") {
                var key = trimmed.substring(2).trim();
                if (key.indexOf(":") > 0) {
                    var parts = key.split(":");
                    var k = parts[0].trim();
                    var v = parts.slice(1).join(":").trim();
                    stack[stack.length - 1].obj[k] = v;
                } else {
                    if (!(stack[stack.length - 1].obj instanceof Array)) stack[stack.length - 1].obj = [];
                    stack[stack.length - 1].obj.push(key);
                }
            } else if (trimmed.indexOf(":") > 0) {
                var colonIndex = trimmed.indexOf(":");
                var key = trimmed.substring(0, colonIndex).trim();
                var value = trimmed.substring(colonIndex + 1).trim();
                if (!value) {
                    var newObj = {};
                    stack[stack.length - 1].obj[key] = newObj;
                    stack.push({ obj: newObj, indent: indent });
                } else {
                    stack[stack.length - 1].obj[key] = value;
                }
            }
        }
        return result;
    };
    this.build = function(data, indent) {
        indent = indent || 0;
        var spaces = "  ".repeat(indent);
        var lines = [];
        for (var key in data) {
            var value = data[key];
            if (typeof value === "object" && value !== null) {
                lines.push(spaces + key + ":");
                lines.push(this.build(value, indent + 1));
            } else {
                lines.push(spaces + key + ": " + value);
            }
        }
        return lines.join("\n");
    };
};
var TOMLParser = function() {
    this.parse = function(toml) {
        var result = {};
        var currentSection = result;
        var lines = toml.split(/\r?\n/);
        for (var i = 0; i < lines.length; i++) {
            var line = lines[i].trim();
            if (!line || line.charAt(0) === "#") continue;
            if (line.charAt(0) === "[" && line.charAt(line.length - 1) === "]") {
                var sectionName = line.substring(1, line.length - 1);
                result[sectionName] = {};
                currentSection = result[sectionName];
            } else if (line.indexOf("=") > 0) {
                var eqIndex = line.indexOf("=");
                var key = line.substring(0, eqIndex).trim();
                var value = line.substring(eqIndex + 1).trim();
                currentSection[key] = this.parseValue(value);
            }
        }
        return result;
    };
    this.parseValue = function(value) {
        value = value.trim();
        if ((value.charAt(0) === "\"" && value.charAt(value.length - 1) === "\"") ||
            (value.charAt(0) === "'" && value.charAt(value.length - 1) === "'")) {
            return value.substring(1, value.length - 1);
        }
        if (value === "true") return true;
        if (value === "false") return false;
        if (value === "null" || value === "nil") return null;
        if (!isNaN(value)) return parseFloat(value);
        return value;
    };
    this.build = function(data) {
        var lines = [];
        for (var key in data) {
            var value = data[key];
            if (typeof value === "object" && value !== null) {
                lines.push("[" + key + "]");
                for (var k in value) lines.push(k + " = " + this.stringifyValue(value[k]));
            } else {
                lines.push(key + " = " + this.stringifyValue(value));
            }
        }
        return lines.join("\n");
    };
    this.stringifyValue = function(value) {
        if (typeof value === "string") return "\"" + value + "\"";
        if (typeof value === "boolean") return value ? "true" : "false";
        if (value === null) return "null";
        return String(value);
    };
};
var MersenneTwister = function(seed) {
    this.N = 624;
    this.M = 397;
    this.MATRIX_A = 0x9908b0df;
    this.UPPER_MASK = 0x80000000;
    this.LOWER_MASK = 0x7fffffff;
    this.mt = new Array(this.N);
    this.mti = this.N + 1;
    this.initializeGenerator = function(seed) {
        this.mt[0] = seed >>> 0;
        for (this.mti = 1; this.mti < this.N; this.mti++) {
            this.mt[this.mti] = (1812433253 * (this.mt[this.mti - 1] ^ (this.mt[this.mti - 1] >>> 30)) + this.mti) >>> 0;
        }
    };
    this.initializeGenerator(seed || Date.now());
    this.extractNumber = function() {
        var y;
        if (this.mti >= this.N) {
            var magk;
            if (this.mti === this.N + 1) this.initializeGenerator(5489);
            for (var kk = 0; kk < this.N - this.M; kk++) {
                y = (this.mt[kk] & this.UPPER_MASK) | (this.mt[kk + 1] & this.LOWER_MASK);
                magk = (y & 0x1) ? this.MATRIX_A : 0;
                this.mt[kk] = this.mt[kk + this.M] ^ (y >>> 1) ^ magk;
            }
            for (; kk < this.N - 1; kk++) {
                y = (this.mt[kk] & this.UPPER_MASK) | (this.mt[kk + 1] & this.LOWER_MASK);
                magk = (y & 0x1) ? this.MATRIX_A : 0;
                this.mt[kk] = this.mt[kk + (this.M - this.N)] ^ (y >>> 1) ^ magk;
            }
            y = (this.mt[this.N - 1] & this.UPPER_MASK) | (this.mt[0] & this.LOWER_MASK);
            magk = (y & 0x1) ? this.MATRIX_A : 0;
            this.mt[this.N - 1] = this.mt[this.M - 1] ^ (y >>> 1) ^ magk;
            this.mti = 0;
        }
        y = this.mt[this.mti++];
        y ^= (y >>> 11);
        y ^= (y << 7) & 0x9d2c5680;
        y ^= (y << 15) & 0xefc60000;
        y ^= (y >>> 18);
        return y >>> 0;
    };
    this.random = function() { return this.extractNumber() / 0xffffffff; };
    this.randomInt = function(min, max) { return Math.floor(this.random() * (max - min + 1)) + min; };
    this.randomFloat = function() { return this.random(); };
    this.randomBoolean = function() { return this.random() < 0.5; };
    this.randomChoice = function(arr) { return arr[this.randomInt(0, arr.length - 1)]; };
    this.randomString = function(length, chars) {
        chars = chars || "ABCDEFGHIJKLMNOPQRSTUVWXYZabcdefghijklmnopqrstuvwxyz0123456789";
        var result = "";
        for (var i = 0; i < length; i++) result += chars.charAt(this.randomInt(0, chars.length - 1));
        return result;
    };
    this.shuffle = function(arr) {
        var result = arr.slice();
        for (var i = result.length - 1; i > 0; i--) {
            var j = this.randomInt(0, i);
            var temp = result[i];
            result[i] = result[j];
            result[j] = temp;
        }
        return result;
    };
};
var LinearCongruentialGenerator = function(a, c, m, seed) {
    this.a = a || 1664525;
    this.c = c || 1013904223;
    this.m = m || 0x100000000;
    this.state = seed || Date.now();
    this.next = function() {
        this.state = (this.a * this.state + this.c) % this.m;
        return this.state / this.m;
    };
    this.random = function() { return this.next(); };
    this.randomInt = function(min, max) { return Math.floor(this.random() * (max - min + 1)) + min; };
    this.reset = function(seed) { this.state = seed || Date.now(); };
};
var Xorshift = function(seed) {
    this.state = seed || Date.now();
    this.next = function() {
        this.state ^= this.state << 13;
        this.state ^= this.state >> 17;
        this.state ^= this.state << 5;
        return this.state >>> 0;
    };
    this.random = function() { return this.next() / 0xffffffff; };
    this.randomInt = function(min, max) { return Math.floor(this.random() * (max - min + 1)) + min; };
};
var PCG = function(seed) {
    this.state = seed || Date.now();
    this.inc = 1283918467;
    this.next = function() {
        var oldstate = this.state;
        this.state = oldstate * 747796405 + this.inc;
        var xorshifted = ((oldstate >>> ((oldstate >>> 28) + 4)) ^ oldstate) * 277803737;
        return (xorshifted >>> 22) ^ xorshifted;
    };
    this.random = function() { return this.next() / 0x10000000; };
    this.randomInt = function(min, max) { return Math.floor(this.random() * (max - min + 1)) + min; };
};
var RandomForest = function(trees, features) {
    this.trees = trees || [];
    this.features = features || [];
    this.numTrees = trees ? trees.length : 100;
    this.maxFeatures = features ? features.length : 0;
    this.train = function(data, options) {
        options = options || {};
        this.numTrees = options.numTrees || 100;
        this.maxFeatures = options.maxFeatures || Math.floor(Math.sqrt(data[0].features.length));
        this.trees = [];
        for (var i = 0; i < this.numTrees; i++) {
            var bootstrapData = this.bootstrapSample(data);
            var dt = new DecisionTreeClassifier();
            dt.train(bootstrapData);
            this.trees.push(dt);
        }
    };
    this.bootstrapSample = function(data) {
        var sample = [];
        for (var i = 0; i < data.length; i++) {
            sample.push(data[Math.floor(Math.random() * data.length)]);
        }
        return sample;
    };
    this.predict = function(sample) {
        var votes = {};
        for (var i = 0; i < this.trees.length; i++) {
            var prediction = this.trees[i].predict(sample);
            votes[prediction] = (votes[prediction] || 0) + 1;
        }
        var bestLabel = null;
        var bestVotes = 0;
        for (var label in votes) {
            if (votes[label] > bestVotes) {
                bestVotes = votes[label];
                bestLabel = label;
            }
        }
        return bestLabel;
    };
};
var NeuralNetwork = function(options) {
    this.layers = options && options.layers ? options.layers : [2, 4, 1];
    this.weights = [];
    this.biases = [];
    this.learningRate = options && options.learningRate ? options.learningRate : 0.1;
    this.initialize = function() {
        for (var i = 0; i < this.layers.length - 1; i++) {
            var layerWeights = [];
            for (var j = 0; j < this.layers[i + 1]; j++) {
                var neuronWeights = [];
                for (var k = 0; k < this.layers[i]; k++) {
                    neuronWeights.push(Math.random() - 0.5);
                }
                layerWeights.push(neuronWeights);
            }
            this.weights.push(layerWeights);
            var layerBiases = [];
            for (var j = 0; j < this.layers[i + 1]; j++) layerBiases.push(Math.random() - 0.5);
            this.biases.push(layerBiases);
        }
    };
    this.initialize();
    this.sigmoid = function(x) { return 1 / (1 + Math.exp(-x)); };
    this.sigmoidDerivative = function(x) { return x * (1 - x); };
    this.forward = function(inputs) {
        var current = inputs.slice();
        this.activations = [current];
        for (var i = 0; i < this.weights.length; i++) {
            var next = [];
            for (var j = 0; j < this.weights[i].length; j++) {
                var sum = this.biases[i][j];
                for (var k = 0; k < current.length; k++) sum += current[k] * this.weights[i][j][k];
                next.push(this.sigmoid(sum));
            }
            current = next;
            this.activations.push(current);
        }
        return current;
    };
    this.backward = function(targets) {
        var numLayers = this.weights.length;
        var deltas = [];
        var outputError = [];
        for (var i = 0; i < targets.length; i++) {
            var output = this.activations[numLayers][i];
            outputError.push(targets[i] - output);
        }
        deltas.unshift(outputError.map(function(e) { return e * this.sigmoidDerivative(this.activations[numLayers][i]); }, { activations: this.activations, sigmoidDerivative: this.sigmoidDerivative }));
        for (var i = numLayers - 1; i > 0; i--) {
            var layerDelta = [];
            for (var j = 0; j < this.weights[i].length; j++) {
                var error = 0;
                for (var k = 0; k < deltas[0].length; k++) {
                    error += deltas[0][k] * this.weights[i][k][j];
                }
                layerDelta.push(error * this.sigmoidDerivative(this.activations[i][j]));
            }
            deltas.unshift(layerDelta);
        }
        for (var i = 0; i < this.weights.length; i++) {
            for (var j = 0; j < this.weights[i].length; j++) {
                for (var k = 0; k < this.weights[i][j].length; k++) {
                    this.weights[i][j][k] += this.learningRate * deltas[i][j] * this.activations[i][k];
                }
                this.biases[i][j] += this.learningRate * deltas[i][j];
            }
        }
    };
    this.train = function(data, epochs) {
        for (var e = 0; e < epochs; e++) {
            for (var i = 0; i < data.length; i++) {
                this.forward(data[i].inputs);
                this.backward(data[i].outputs);
            }
        }
    };
    this.predict = function(inputs) { return this.forward(inputs); };
};
var LogisticRegressionClassifier = function() {
    this.weights = [];
    this.bias = 0;
    this.learningRate = 0.1;
    this.train = function(data, iterations) {
        var numFeatures = data[0].inputs.length;
        this.weights = new Array(numFeatures).fill(0);
        this.bias = 0;
        for (var iter = 0; iter < iterations; iter++) {
            for (var i = 0; i < data.length; i++) {
                var output = this.predictOne(data[i].inputs);
                var error = data[i].outputs[0] - output;
                for (var j = 0; j < numFeatures; j++) {
                    this.weights[j] += this.learningRate * error * output * (1 - output) * data[i].inputs[j];
                }
                this.bias += this.learningRate * error * output * (1 - output);
            }
        }
    };
    this.sigmoid = function(x) { return 1 / (1 + Math.exp(-x)); };
    this.predictOne = function(inputs) {
        var sum = this.bias;
        for (var i = 0; i < inputs.length; i++) sum += inputs[i] * this.weights[i];
        return this.sigmoid(sum);
    };
    this.predict = function(inputs) {
        return this.predictOne(inputs) > 0.5 ? 1 : 0;
    };
};
var KNearestNeighbors = function(k) {
    this.k = k || 3;
    this.data = [];
    this.train = function(data) { this.data = data; };
    this.distance = function(a, b) {
        var sum = 0;
        for (var i = 0; i < a.length; i++) sum += Math.pow(a[i] - b[i], 2);
        return Math.sqrt(sum);
    };
    this.predict = function(point) {
        var distances = [];
        for (var i = 0; i < this.data.length; i++) {
            distances.push({ dist: this.distance(point, this.data[i].features), label: this.data[i].label });
        }
        distances.sort(function(a, b) { return a.dist - b.dist; });
        var votes = {};
        for (var i = 0; i < this.k; i++) {
            votes[distances[i].label] = (votes[distances[i].label] || 0) + 1;
        }
        var bestLabel = null;
        var bestVotes = 0;
        for (var label in votes) {
            if (votes[label] > bestVotes) {
                bestVotes = votes[label];
                bestLabel = label;
            }
        }
        return bestLabel;
    };
};
var SupportVectorMachine = function() {
    this.weights = [];
    this.bias = 0;
    this.learningRate = 0.1;
    this.train = function(data, iterations) {
        var numFeatures = data[0].features.length;
        this.weights = new Array(numFeatures).fill(0);
        this.bias = 0;
        for (var iter = 0; iter < iterations; iter++) {
            for (var i = 0; i < data.length; i++) {
                var label = data[i].label * 2 - 1;
                var score = this.bias;
                for (var j = 0; j < numFeatures; j++) score += this.weights[j] * data[i].features[j];
                if (label * score < 1) {
                    for (var j = 0; j < numFeatures; j++) this.weights[j] += this.learningRate * label * data[i].features[j];
                    this.bias += this.learningRate * label;
                }
            }
        }
    };
    this.predictOne = function(features) {
        var score = this.bias;
        for (var i = 0; i < features.length; i++) score += this.weights[i] * features[i];
        return score > 0 ? 1 : 0;
    };
    this.predict = function(features) { return this.predictOne(features); };
};
var AdalineClassifier = function() {
    this.weights = [];
    this.bias = 0;
    this.learningRate = 0.01;
    this.train = function(data, epochs) {
        var numFeatures = data[0].features.length;
        this.weights = new Array(numFeatures).fill(0);
        this.bias = 0;
        for (var e = 0; e < epochs; e++) {
            for (var i = 0; i < data.length; i++) {
                var output = this.bias;
                for (var j = 0; j < numFeatures; j++) output += this.weights[j] * data[i].features[j];
                var error = data[i].label - output;
                for (var j = 0; j < numFeatures; j++) this.weights[j] += this.learningRate * error * data[i].features[j];
                this.bias += this.learningRate * error;
            }
        }
    };
    this.predict = function(features) {
        var output = this.bias;
        for (var i = 0; i < features.length; i++) output += this.weights[i] * features[i];
        return output > 0 ? 1 : 0;
    };
};
var Perceptron = function() {
    this.weights = [];
    this.bias = 0;
    this.learningRate = 0.1;
    this.train = function(data, epochs) {
        var numFeatures = data[0].features.length;
        this.weights = new Array(numFeatures).fill(0);
        this.bias = 0;
        for (var e = 0; e < epochs; e++) {
            for (var i = 0; i < data.length; i++) {
                var output = this.bias;
                for (var j = 0; j < numFeatures; j++) output += this.weights[j] * data[i].features[j];
                var prediction = output > 0 ? 1 : 0;
                var error = data[i].label - prediction;
                for (var j = 0; j < numFeatures; j++) this.weights[j] += this.learningRate * error * data[i].features[j];
                this.bias += this.learningRate * error;
            }
        }
    };
    this.predict = function(features) {
        var sum = this.bias;
        for (var i = 0; i < features.length; i++) sum += this.weights[i] * features[i];
        return sum > 0 ? 1 : 0;
    };
};
var HebbianRule = function() {
    this.weights = [];
    this.learningRate = 0.1;
    this.train = function(data, iterations) {
        var numFeatures = data[0].features.length;
        this.weights = new Array(numFeatures).fill(0);
        for (var iter = 0; iter < iterations; iter++) {
            for (var i = 0; i < data.length; i++) {
                var output = 0;
                for (var j = 0; j < numFeatures; j++) output += this.weights[j] * data[i].features[j];
                for (var j = 0; j < numFeatures; j++) this.weights[j] += this.learningRate * data[i].features[j] * output;
            }
        }
    };
    this.predict = function(features) {
        var sum = 0;
        for (var i = 0; i < features.length; i++) sum += this.weights[i] * features[i];
        return sum > 0 ? 1 : 0;
    };
};
var OjaRule = function() {
    this.weights = [];
    this.learningRate = 0.01;
    this.train = function(data, iterations) {
        var numFeatures = data[0].features.length;
        this.weights = new Array(numFeatures).fill(0);
        for (var iter = 0; iter < iterations; iter++) {
            for (var i = 0; i < data.length; i++) {
                var output = 0;
                for (var j = 0; j < numFeatures; j++) output += this.weights[j] * data[i].features[j];
                for (var j = 0; j < numFeatures; j++) {
                    this.weights[j] += this.learningRate * (data[i].features[j] * output - output * output * this.weights[j]);
                }
            }
        }
    };
    this.predict = function(features) {
        var sum = 0;
        for (var i = 0; i < features.length; i++) sum += this.weights[i] * features[i];
        return sum > 0 ? 1 : 0;
    };
};
var SOMNeuron = function(x, y, dimensions) {
    this.x = x;
    this.y = y;
    this.weights = [];
    for (var i = 0; i < dimensions; i++) this.weights.push(Math.random());
    this.distanceTo = function(other) {
        return Math.sqrt(Math.pow(this.x - other.x, 2) + Math.pow(this.y - other.y, 2));
    };
};
var SelfOrganizingMap = function(width, height, dimensions) {
    this.width = width || 10;
    this.height = height || 10;
    this.dimensions = dimensions || 2;
    this.neurons = [];
    this.learningRate = 0.5;
    this.radius = Math.max(width, height) / 2;
    this.initialize = function() {
        for (var x = 0; x < this.width; x++) {
            for (var y = 0; y < this.height; y++) {
                this.neurons.push(new SOMNeuron(x, y, this.dimensions));
            }
        }
    };
    this.initialize();
    this.findBestMatchingUnit = function(input) {
        var best = null;
        var bestDistance = Infinity;
        for (var i = 0; i < this.neurons.length; i++) {
            var dist = 0;
            for (var j = 0; j < input.length; j++) dist += Math.pow(input[j] - this.neurons[i].weights[j], 2);
            if (dist < bestDistance) {
                bestDistance = dist;
                best = this.neurons[i];
            }
        }
        return best;
    };
    this.train = function(data, iterations) {
        for (var iter = 0; iter < iterations; iter++) {
            var t = iter / iterations;
            var currentRadius = this.radius * Math.exp(-t);
            var currentLR = this.learningRate * Math.exp(-t);
            for (var i = 0; i < data.length; i++) {
                var bmu = this.findBestMatchingUnit(data[i]);
                for (var j = 0; j < this.neurons.length; j++) {
                    var dist = this.neurons[j].distanceTo(bmu);
                    if (dist < currentRadius) {
                        var influence = Math.exp(-dist * dist / (2 * currentRadius * currentRadius));
                        for (var k = 0; k < this.neurons[j].weights.length; k++) {
                            this.neurons[j].weights[k] += currentLR * influence * (data[i][k] - this.neurons[j].weights[k]);
                        }
                    }
                }
            }
        }
    };
};
var BoltzmannMachine = function(numVisible, numHidden) {
    this.numVisible = numVisible;
    this.numHidden = numHidden;
    this.weights = [];
    this.visibleBias = [];
    this.hiddenBias = [];
    this.initialize = function() {
        for (var i = 0; i < numVisible + numHidden; i++) {
            this.weights.push([]);
            for (var j = 0; j < numVisible + numHidden; j++) {
                this.weights[i].push(Math.random() - 0.5);
            }
        }
        for (var i = 0; i < numVisible; i++) this.visibleBias.push(Math.random() - 0.5);
        for (var i = 0; i < numHidden; i++) this.hiddenBias.push(Math.random() - 0.5);
    };
    this.initialize();
    this.sigmoid = function(x) { return 1 / (1 + Math.exp(-x)); };
    this.probability = function(neuron, active) {
        var sum = neuron < this.numVisible ? this.visibleBias[neuron] : this.hiddenBias[neuron - this.numVisible];
        for (var i = 0; i < this.weights[neuron].length; i++) {
            sum += this.weights[neuron][i] * (i < this.numVisible ? active[i] : active[i - this.numVisible]);
        }
        return this.sigmoid(sum);
    };
    this.sample = function(active, iterations) {
        for (var iter = 0; iter < iterations; iter++) {
            for (var i = 0; i < this.numVisible + this.numHidden; i++) {
                active[i] = Math.random() < this.probability(i, active) ? 1 : 0;
            }
        }
        return active;
    };
};
var RestrictedBoltzmannMachine = function(numVisible, numHidden) {
    this.numVisible = numVisible;
    this.numHidden = numHidden;
    this.weights = [];
    this.visibleBias = [];
    this.hiddenBias = [];
    this.learningRate = 0.1;
    this.initialize = function() {
        for (var i = 0; i < numVisible; i++) {
            this.weights.push([]);
            for (var j = 0; j < numHidden; j++) this.weights[i].push(Math.random() - 0.5);
        }
        for (var i = 0; i < numVisible; i++) this.visibleBias.push(0);
        for (var j = 0; j < numHidden; j++) this.hiddenBias.push(0);
    };
    this.initialize();
    this.sampleHidden = function(visible) {
        var hiddenProb = [];
        for (var j = 0; j < this.numHidden; j++) {
            var prob = 0;
            for (var i = 0; i < this.numVisible; i++) prob += visible[i] * this.weights[i][j];
            prob += this.hiddenBias[j];
            hiddenProb.push(1 / (1 + Math.exp(-prob)));
        }
        return hiddenProb.map(function(p) { return Math.random() < p ? 1 : 0; });
    };
    this.sampleVisible = function(hidden) {
        var visibleProb = [];
        for (var i = 0; i < this.numVisible; i++) {
            var prob = 0;
            for (var j = 0; j < this.numHidden; j++) prob += hidden[j] * this.weights[i][j];
            prob += this.visibleBias[i];
            visibleProb.push(1 / (1 + Math.exp(-prob)));
        }
        return visibleProb.map(function(p) { return Math.random() < p ? 1 : 0; });
    };
    this.train = function(data, epochs) {
        for (var e = 0; e < epochs; e++) {
            for (var i = 0; i < data.length; i++) {
                var v0 = data[i];
                var h0 = this.sampleHidden(v0);
                var v1 = this.sampleVisible(h0);
                var h1 = this.sampleHidden(v1);
                for (var j = 0; j < this.numVisible; j++) {
                    for (var k = 0; k < this.numHidden; k++) {
                        this.weights[j][k] += this.learningRate * (v0[j] * h0[k] - v1[j] * h1[k]);
                    }
                }
            }
        }
    };
};
var HashTableImplementation = function(size) {
    this.size = size || 100;
    this.buckets = [];
    this.initialize = function() {
        for (var i = 0; i < this.size; i++) this.buckets.push([]);
    };
    this.initialize();
    this.hash = function(key) {
        var hash = 0;
        for (var i = 0; i < key.length; i++) hash = ((hash << 5) - hash) + key.charCodeAt(i);
        return Math.abs(hash) % this.size;
    };
    this.put = function(key, value) {
        var index = this.hash(key);
        var bucket = this.buckets[index];
        for (var i = 0; i < bucket.length; i++) {
            if (bucket[i].key === key) { bucket[i].value = value; return; }
        }
        bucket.push({ key: key, value: value });
    };
    this.get = function(key) {
        var index = this.hash(key);
        var bucket = this.buckets[index];
        for (var i = 0; i < bucket.length; i++) {
            if (bucket[i].key === key) return bucket[i].value;
        }
        return null;
    };
    this.remove = function(key) {
        var index = this.hash(key);
        var bucket = this.buckets[index];
        for (var i = 0; i < bucket.length; i++) {
            if (bucket[i].key === key) { bucket.splice(i, 1); return true; }
        }
        return false;
    };
    this.contains = function(key) { return this.get(key) !== null; };
    this.keys = function() {
        var keys = [];
        for (var i = 0; i < this.buckets.length; i++) {
            for (var j = 0; j < this.buckets[i].length; j++) keys.push(this.buckets[i][j].key);
        }
        return keys;
    };
    this.values = function() {
        var values = [];
        for (var i = 0; i < this.buckets.length; i++) {
            for (var j = 0; j < this.buckets[i].length; j++) values.push(this.buckets[i][j].value);
        }
        return values;
    };
    this.size = function() { return this.keys().length; };
    this.isEmpty = function() { return this.size() === 0; };
    this.clear = function() { this.initialize(); };
};
var HashSetImplementation = function(size) {
    this.size = size || 100;
    this.buckets = [];
    this.initialize = function() {
        for (var i = 0; i < this.size; i++) this.buckets.push([]);
    };
    this.initialize();
    this.hash = function(item) {
        var str = String(item);
        var hash = 0;
        for (var i = 0; i < str.length; i++) hash = ((hash << 5) - hash) + str.charCodeAt(i);
        return Math.abs(hash) % this.size;
    };
    this.add = function(item) {
        var index = this.hash(item);
        var bucket = this.buckets[index];
        for (var i = 0; i < bucket.length; i++) {
            if (bucket[i] === item) return;
        }
        bucket.push(item);
    };
    this.contains = function(item) {
        var index = this.hash(item);
        var bucket = this.buckets[index];
        for (var i = 0; i < bucket.length; i++) {
            if (bucket[i] === item) return true;
        }
        return false;
    };
    this.remove = function(item) {
        var index = this.hash(item);
        var bucket = this.buckets[index];
        for (var i = 0; i < bucket.length; i++) {
            if (bucket[i] === item) { bucket.splice(i, 1); return true; }
        }
        return false;
    };
    this.toArray = function() {
        var result = [];
        for (var i = 0; i < this.buckets.length; i++) {
            for (var j = 0; j < this.buckets[i].length; j++) result.push(this.buckets[i][j]);
        }
        return result;
    };
    this.size = function() { return this.toArray().length; };
    this.isEmpty = function() { return this.size() === 0; };
    this.clear = function() { this.initialize(); };
};
var LinkedListImplementation = function() {
    this.head = null;
    this.tail = null;
    this.size = 0;
    this.Node = function(value) {
        this.value = value;
        this.next = null;
        this.prev = null;
    };
    this.append = function(value) {
        var node = new this.Node(value);
        if (!this.head) { this.head = node; this.tail = node; }
        else { node.prev = this.tail; this.tail.next = node; this.tail = node; }
        this.size++;
    };
    this.prepend = function(value) {
        var node = new this.Node(value);
        if (!this.head) { this.head = node; this.tail = node; }
        else { node.next = this.head; this.head.prev = node; this.head = node; }
        this.size++;
    };
    this.getAt = function(index) {
        if (index < 0 || index >= this.size) return null;
        var current = this.head;
        for (var i = 0; i < index; i++) current = current.next;
        return current.value;
    };
    this.insertAt = function(index, value) {
        if (index < 0 || index > this.size) return false;
        if (index === 0) { this.prepend(value); return true; }
        if (index === this.size) { this.append(value); return true; }
        var node = new this.Node(value);
        var prev = this.head;
        for (var i = 0; i < index - 1; i++) prev = prev.next;
        node.next = prev.next;
        node.prev = prev;
        prev.next.prev = node;
        prev.next = node;
        this.size++;
        return true;
    };
    this.removeAt = function(index) {
        if (index < 0 || index >= this.size) return null;
        var current = this.head;
        for (var i = 0; i < index; i++) current = current.next;
        if (current.prev) current.prev.next = current.next;
        else this.head = current.next;
        if (current.next) current.next.prev = current.prev;
        else this.tail = current.prev;
        this.size--;
        return current.value;
    };
    this.remove = function(value) {
        var current = this.head;
        while (current) {
            if (current.value === value) {
                if (current.prev) current.prev.next = current.next;
                else this.head = current.next;
                if (current.next) current.next.prev = current.prev;
                else this.tail = current.prev;
                this.size--;
                return true;
            }
            current = current.next;
        }
        return false;
    };
    this.indexOf = function(value) {
        var current = this.head;
        var index = 0;
        while (current) {
            if (current.value === value) return index;
            current = current.next;
            index++;
        }
        return -1;
    };
    this.contains = function(value) { return this.indexOf(value) >= 0; };
    this.toArray = function() {
        var result = [];
        var current = this.head;
        while (current) { result.push(current.value); current = current.next; }
        return result;
    };
    this.clear = function() { this.head = null; this.tail = null; this.size = 0; };
};
